Add _last_notif_time and update _app_show_notification to rate limit notifications and hoist allocations before posix.fork()
--- REPLACEMENT:
_last_notif_time: time.Time

_app_show_notification :: proc(title: string, message: string) {
	when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
		now := time.now()
		if _last_notif_time._nsec != 0 && time.diff(_last_notif_time, now) < 1 * time.Second {
			return
		}
		_last_notif_time = now

		c_prog := strings.clone_to_cstring("/usr/bin/osascript", context.temp_allocator)
		c_e := strings.clone_to_cstring("-e", context.temp_allocator)
		c_script := strings.clone_to_cstring("on run {msg, ttl}\ndisplay notification msg with title ttl\nend run", context.temp_allocator)
		c_msg := strings.clone_to_cstring(message, context.temp_allocator)
		c_ttl := strings.clone_to_cstring(title, context.temp_allocator)
		c_argv := [6]cstring{c_prog, c_e, c_script, c_msg, c_ttl, nil}

		pid := posix.fork()
		if pid == 0 {
			if posix.fork() == 0 {
				posix.close(0)
				posix.close(1)
				posix.close(2)
				posix.execvp(c_prog, raw_data(c_argv[:]))
				posix._exit(1)
			}
			posix._exit(0)
		}
		if pid > 0 {
			posix.waitpid(pid, nil, {})
		}
	}
}

import config "../config"
import inter "../interaction"
import ui "../ui"

// App composes Multi-Session Manager, UI Chrome, and Frontend subsystems.
App :: struct {
	session_mgr:    Session_Manager,
	using backend:  Backend, // Active tab backend fallback for tests/headless
	using frontend: Frontend,
	tab_bar:        ui.Tab_Bar_State,
	tab_rects:      [MAX_TABS]ui.Rect_f32,
	search_bar:     ui.Search_Bar_State,
	ui_theme:       ui.UI_Theme,
	ui_style:       ui.UI_Style,
	base_title:     string,
	hud_active:     bool,
	should_quit:    bool,
}

// app_active_backend resolves the currently active session backend.
app_active_backend :: proc(a: ^App) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		return &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx].backend
	}
	return &a.backend
}

// app_global holds the active App pointer for callbacks.
app_global: ^App

_app_response_cb :: _backend_response_cb
_app_clipboard_cb :: _backend_clipboard_cb
_app_clipboard_read_cb :: _backend_clipboard_read_cb

// app_init initializes Frontend and Backend subsystems in order.
app_init :: proc(a: ^App, rows, cols: int, prog: string, argv: []string) -> bool {

		valid := 0
		for i in 0..<len(a.renderer.atlas.slots) {
			if a.renderer.atlas.slots[i].valid {
				valid += 1
			}
		}
		fmt.eprintf(
			"app_debug: init rows=%d cols=%d atlas_valid=%d/%d surface=%dx%d strategy=%v font='%s'\n",
			active_b.terminal.grid.row_count,
			active_b.terminal.grid.col_count,
			valid,
			len(a.renderer.atlas.slots),
			a.renderer.surface_w,
			a.renderer.surface_h,
			a.renderer.strategy,
			a.font_path,
		)
	}

	_ = sdl3.AddEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)
	_ = backend_start_thread(active_b)
	return true
}

// app_destroy tears down Session Manager and Frontend in order.
app_destroy :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil {
		return
	}
	sdl3.RemoveEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)
	session_manager_destroy(&a.session_mgr)
	if a.backend.drain_buf != nil {
		backend_destroy(&a.backend)
	}
	frontend_destroy(&a.frontend)
	config.config_destroy(&a.config)
}

[b8c42ddd-324b-495f-b775-345903fded7a] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin

_app_sync_focus :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil {
		return
	}
	flags := sdl3.GetWindowFlags(a.window.handle)
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b != nil {
		backend_set_focused(b, .INPUT_FOCUS in flags)
	}
}

_app_pointer_cell :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	if a == nil {
		return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	return frontend_pointer_cell(&a.frontend, b.terminal.grid.row_count, b.terminal.grid.col_count, x, y)
}

_app_pointer_point :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	viewport := _app_pointer_cell(a, x, y)
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	return termgrid.terminal_view_point_from_viewport(
		&b.terminal,
		&b.view,
		viewport,
	)

_app_pointer_wheel_delta :: backend_pointer_wheel_delta
_app_pointer_selection_changed :: backend_pointer_selection_changed

_app_route_pointer :: proc(a: ^App, pointer: input.Input_Pointer_Event) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false

	if b.terminal.mouse_tracking != .None && !pointer.shift {
		pt := _app_pointer_cell(a, pointer.x, pointer.y)
		col := clamp(pt.col + 1, 1, max(1, b.terminal.grid.col_count))
		row := clamp(pt.row + 1, 1, max(1, b.terminal.grid.row_count))

		if pointer.kind == .Motion {
			if b.terminal.mouse_tracking == .Normal {
				return true
			}
			if b.terminal.mouse_tracking == .Button_Event && !pointer.primary_down && pointer.button == 0 {
				return true
			}
			if col == b.last_mouse_col && row == b.last_mouse_row {
				return true
			}
		}

		buf: [32]u8
		n := input.mouse_encode_sgr(pointer, col, row, pointer.shift, buf[:])
		if n > 0 {
			_ = pty.pty_write(&b.pty, buf[:n])
		}
		b.last_mouse_col = col
		b.last_mouse_row = row
		return true
	}

	pt_viewport := _app_pointer_cell(a, pointer.x, pointer.y)
	doc_pt := termgrid.terminal_view_point_from_viewport(&b.terminal, &b.view, pt_viewport)
	p_doc := pointer
	p_doc.x = f32(doc_pt.col * APP_CELL_W)
	p_doc.y = f32(doc_pt.row * APP_CELL_H)
	total_doc_rows := termgrid.scrollback_len(&b.terminal.scrollback) + b.terminal.grid.row_count

	consumed, action := inter.interaction_dispatch_pointer(
		&b.interaction,
		p_doc,
		b.terminal.is_alt_screen,
		total_doc_rows,
		b.terminal.grid.col_count,
		APP_CELL_W,
		APP_CELL_H,
	)

	if consumed {
		if pointer.kind == .Button_Down && pointer.button == 1 {
			clicks := pointer.clicks == 0 ? 1 : pointer.clicks
			if clicks == 2 {
				start, end := inter.interaction_select_word_bounds(&b.terminal, doc_pt)
				b.interaction.selection_anchor = start
				b.interaction.visual_cursor = end
			} else if clicks >= 3 {
				start, end := inter.interaction_select_line_bounds(&b.terminal, doc_pt)
				b.interaction.selection_anchor = start
				b.interaction.visual_cursor = end
			}
		}

		switch action {
		case .Copy:
			text := inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&b.terminal, &b.interaction)
			if len(text) > 0 {
				_app_clipboard_cb(transmute([]u8)text)
				delete(text)
			}
			termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&b.view, &b.terminal, 0)
			b.view_generation += 1
		case .Resume_Live:
			termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&b.view, &b.terminal, 0)
			b.view_generation += 1
		case .Scroll_To_Match:
			if b.interaction.search_active && b.interaction.search_match_count > 0 {
				match_row := b.interaction.search_matches[b.interaction.search_match_idx].row
				sb_len := termgrid.scrollback_len(&b.terminal.scrollback)
				target_offset := max(0, sb_len - match_row)
				termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&b.view, &b.terminal, target_offset)
				b.view_generation += 1
			}
		case .Paste, .None:
		case .Open_Link:
			term_ref := &b.front_terminal if backend_is_threaded(b) else &b.terminal
			target := inter.interaction_detect_link_at_point(term_ref, doc_pt)
			if target.kind == .URL {
				url_str := string(target.text[:target.len])
				_ = sdl3.OpenURL(fmt.ctprintf("%s", url_str))
			} else if target.kind == .Path {
				path_str := string(target.text[:target.len])
				clean_path := path_str
				colon_idx := strings.index(path_str, ":")
				if colon_idx > 0 {
					clean_path = path_str[:colon_idx]
				}
				if strings.has_prefix(clean_path, "~/") {
					home := os.get_env("HOME", context.temp_allocator)
					clean_path = fmt.tprintf("%s/%s", home, clean_path[2:])
				}
				when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
					c_prog := strings.clone_to_cstring("/usr/bin/open", context.temp_allocator)
					c_opt := strings.clone_to_cstring("--", context.temp_allocator)
					c_path := strings.clone_to_cstring(clean_path, context.temp_allocator)
					c_argv := [4]cstring{c_prog, c_opt, c_path, nil}
					pid := posix.fork()
					if pid == 0 {
						if posix.fork() == 0 {
							posix.close(0)
							posix.close(1)
							posix.close(2)
							posix.execvp(c_prog, raw_data(c_argv[:]))
							posix._exit(1)
						}
						posix._exit(0)
					}
					if pid > 0 {
						posix.waitpid(pid, nil, {})
					}
				}
			}
		}

		b.view.selection.active = b.interaction.selection_active
		b.view.selection.anchor = b.interaction.selection_anchor
		b.view.selection.focus = b.interaction.visual_cursor
		b.view.selection.block = (b.interaction.visual_kind == .Block)
		b.view.visual_mode = (b.interaction.mode == .Visual)
		b.view_generation += 1
		return true
	}

	switch pointer.kind {
	case .Wheel:
		if b.terminal.is_alt_screen {
			delta := _app_pointer_wheel_delta(pointer)
			if delta == 0 {
				return true
			}
			steps := abs(delta) * APP_ALT_SCREEN_WHEEL_LINES
			code: u8 = 'A' if delta > 0 else 'B'
			seq: [3]u8
			if b.terminal.app_cursor_keys {
				seq = {0x1B, 'O', code}
			} else {
				seq = {0x1B, '[', code}
			}
			for _ in 0 ..< steps {
				_ = pty.pty_write(&b.pty, seq[:])
			}
			return true
		}
		delta := _app_pointer_wheel_delta(pointer)
		old_offset := b.view.scrollback_offset
		_ = termgrid.terminal_view_scroll(&b.view, &b.terminal, delta)
		if old_offset != b.view.scrollback_offset {
			if b.view.scrollback_offset > 0 && b.interaction.viewport_flow == .Live {
				inter.interaction_pause_viewport(&b.interaction, b.view.scrollback_offset)
			} else if b.view.scrollback_offset == 0 && b.interaction.mode == .Passthrough {
				inter.interaction_resume_viewport(&b.interaction)
			}
			b.view_generation += 1
		}
	case .Button_Down:
		if pointer.button != 1 {
			return true
		}
		point := _app_pointer_point(a, pointer.x, pointer.y)
		_ = _app_pointer_selection_changed(b, point, true)
		_ = win.window_capture_mouse(&a.window, true)
	case .Motion:
		if b.view.selection.active && b.view.selection.anchor != b.view.selection.focus && !pointer.primary_down {
			return true
		}
		if b.view.selection.active && pointer.primary_down {
			point := _app_pointer_point(a, pointer.x, pointer.y)
			_ = _app_pointer_selection_changed(b, point, false)
		}
	case .Button_Up:
		if pointer.button != 1 {
			return true
		}
		if b.view.selection.active {
			point := _app_pointer_point(a, pointer.x, pointer.y)
			_ = _app_pointer_selection_changed(b, point, false)
		}
		_ = win.window_capture_mouse(&a.window, false)
	}
	return true
}

_app_copy_selection :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	copied: string
	if backend_is_threaded(b) {
		if b.front_interaction.selection_active {
			copied = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&b.front_terminal, &b.front_interaction)
		} else {
			copied = backend_copy_selection(b)
		}
	} else {
		if b.interaction.selection_active {
			copied = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&b.terminal, &b.interaction)
		} else {
			copied = backend_copy_selection(b)
		}
	}
	defer delete(copied)
	if len(copied) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	return win.window_set_clipboard_text(&a.window, copied)
}

_app_paste_clipboard :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if a == nil || b == nil || b.pty.state == .Exited {
		return false
	}
	text := win.window_get_clipboard_text(&a.window)
	if len(text) == 0 {
		delete(text)
		return true
	}
	if backend_is_threaded(b) {
		return backend_push_event(b, UI_Event{type = .Paste, text = text})
	}
	defer delete(text)
	return backend_paste(b, text)
}

_app_request_zoom :: proc(a: ^App, direction: int) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	return frontend_request_zoom(&a.frontend, b.pty.state, direction)
}

_app_apply_zoom :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	return frontend_apply_zoom(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty)
}

_app_copy_selection :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	copied: string
	if backend_is_threaded(&a.backend) {
		if a.backend.front_interaction.selection_active {
			copied = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&a.backend.front_terminal, &a.backend.front_interaction)
		} else {
			copied = backend_copy_selection(&a.backend)
		}
	} else {
		if a.backend.interaction.selection_active {
			copied = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&a.backend.terminal, &a.backend.interaction)
		} else {
			copied = backend_copy_selection(&a.backend)
		}
	}
	defer delete(copied)
	if len(copied) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	return win.window_set_clipboard_text(&a.window, copied)
}

_app_paste_clipboard :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil || a.pty.state == .Exited {
		return false
	}
	text := win.window_get_clipboard_text(&a.window)
	if len(text) == 0 {
		delete(text)
		return true
	}
	if backend_is_threaded(&a.backend) {
		return backend_push_event(&a.backend, UI_Event{type = .Paste, text = text})
	}
	defer delete(text)
	return backend_paste(&a.backend, text)
}

_app_request_zoom :: proc(a: ^App, direction: int) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	return frontend_request_zoom(&a.frontend, a.pty.state, direction)
}

_app_apply_zoom :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	return frontend_apply_zoom(&a.frontend, &a.terminal, &a.pty)
}

[b8c42ddd-324b-495f-b775-345903fded7a] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update app_dispatch_input_events with hotkeys and UI routing
--- REPLACEMENT:

	if new_cfg.font_size > 0 && new_cfg.font_size != a.config.font_size {

app_dispatch_input_events :: proc(a: ^App, evs: []input.Input_Event) -> (quit: bool, ok: bool) {
	if a == nil {
		return true, false
	}
	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil {
		return true, false
	}
	key_evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
	key_count := 0
	ok = true
	for ev in evs {
		// (1) Global Hotkey Router
		if ev.event_type == .Key && ev.gui && !ev.is_release {
			if ev.rune == 't' || ev.rune == 'T' {
				shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
				shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
				rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
				cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
				new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
				if spawn_ok {
					new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
					backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
					_ = backend_start_thread(new_b)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'w' || ev.rune == 'W' {
				session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, a.session_mgr.active_idx)
				if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
					a.should_quit = true
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'f' || ev.rune == 'F' {
				a.search_bar.visible = !a.search_bar.visible
				if a.search_bar.visible {
					ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w))
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune >= '1' && ev.rune <= '9' {
				target_tab := int(ev.rune - '1')
				session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_tab)
				continue
			}
		}

		// (2) Search Bar Key Dispatch
		if a.search_bar.visible && ev.event_type == .Key {
			consumed, s_action := ui.search_bar_dispatch_key(&a.search_bar, ev)
			if consumed {
				switch s_action {
				case .Query_Changed:
					var_matches: [inter.MAX_SEARCH_MATCHES]inter.Search_Match
					_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, var_matches[:])
				case .Next_Match, .Prev_Match:
					if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
						if s_action == .Next_Match {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx + 1) % a.search_bar.match_count
						} else {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx - 1 + a.search_bar.match_count) % a.search_bar.match_count
						}
					}
				case .Close:
					a.search_bar.visible = false
				case .None:
				}
				continue
			}
		}

		switch ev.event_type {
		case .Key:
			state_before := active_b.interaction
			consumed, action := inter.interaction_dispatch_key(&active_b.interaction, ev, active_b.terminal.is_alt_screen)
			if consumed {
				if active_b.interaction.search_active {
					q := string(active_b.interaction.search_query[:active_b.interaction.search_len])
					active_b.interaction.search_match_count = inter.interaction_search_scan(
						&active_b.terminal,
						q,
						active_b.interaction.search_matches[:],
					)
					if active_b.interaction.search_match_idx >= active_b.interaction.search_match_count {
						active_b.interaction.search_match_idx = 0
					}
				}
				switch action {
				case .Copy:
					text := inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&active_b.terminal, &active_b.interaction)
					if len(text) == 0 && state_before.selection_active {
						text = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&active_b.terminal, &state_before)
					}
					if len(text) > 0 {
						_app_clipboard_cb(transmute([]u8)text)
						delete(text)
					}
					termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, 0)
					active_b.view_generation += 1
				case .Resume_Live:
					termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, 0)
					active_b.view_generation += 1
				case .Scroll_To_Match:
					if active_b.interaction.search_active && active_b.interaction.search_match_count > 0 {
						match_row := active_b.interaction.search_matches[active_b.interaction.search_match_idx].row
						sb_len := termgrid.scrollback_len(&active_b.terminal.scrollback)
						target_offset := max(0, sb_len - match_row)
						termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, target_offset)
						active_b.view_generation += 1
					}
				case .Paste:
					buf: [4096]u8
					n := _app_clipboard_read_cb(active_b, buf[:])
					if n > 0 {
						backend_paste(active_b, string(buf[:n]))
					}
				case .None, .Open_Link:
				}
				active_b.view.selection.active = active_b.interaction.selection_active
				active_b.view.selection.anchor = active_b.interaction.selection_anchor
				active_b.view.selection.focus = active_b.interaction.visual_cursor
				active_b.view.selection.block = (active_b.interaction.visual_kind == .Block)
				active_b.view.visual_mode = (active_b.interaction.mode == .Visual)
				active_b.view_generation += 1
				continue
			}

			if active_b.pty.state == .Exited {
				switch app_handle_exited_key(ev) {
				case .Relaunch:
					_ = app_relaunch(a)
				case .Quit:
					a.should_quit = true
				case .None:
				}
			} else if key_count < len(key_evs) {
				key_evs[key_count] = ev
				key_count += 1
			}
		case .Local:
			switch ev.action {
			case .Copy:
				if !_app_copy_selection(a) {
					ok = false
				}
			case .Paste:
				if !_app_paste_clipboard(a) {
					ok = false
				}
			case .Zoom_In:
				_ = _app_request_zoom(a, 1)
			case .Zoom_Out:
				_ = _app_request_zoom(a, -1)
			case .Reload_Config:
				_ = app_reload_config(a)
			case .None:
			}
		case .Pointer:
			// Pointer Hit & Focus Gate
			px := ev.pointer.x
			py := ev.pointer.y

			// (a) Search Bar Hit
			if a.search_bar.visible && ui.point_in_rect(px, py, a.search_bar.rect) {
				consumed, s_action := ui.search_bar_dispatch_pointer(&a.search_bar, px, py, ev.pointer.kind == .Button_Down)
				if consumed {
					switch s_action {
					case .Query_Changed:
						var_matches: [inter.MAX_SEARCH_MATCHES]inter.Search_Match
						_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, var_matches[:])
					case .Next_Match, .Prev_Match:
						if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
							if s_action == .Next_Match {
								a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx + 1) % a.search_bar.match_count
							} else {
								a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx - 1 + a.search_bar.match_count) % a.search_bar.match_count
							}
						}
					case .Close:
						a.search_bar.visible = false
					case .None:
					}
					continue
				}
			}

			// (b) Tab Bar Hit
			if py < ui.TAB_BAR_HEIGHT {
				tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
				consumed, t_action, target_idx := ui.tab_bar_dispatch_pointer(
					&a.tab_bar,
					tab_count,
					a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
					px,
					py,
					ev.pointer.kind == .Button_Down,
				)
				if consumed {
					switch t_action {
					case .Switch_Tab:
						session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_idx)
					case .Close_Tab:
						session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_idx)
						if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
							a.should_quit = true
						}
					case .New_Tab:
						shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
						shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
						rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
						cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
						new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
						if spawn_ok {
							new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
							backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
							_ = backend_start_thread(new_b)
							session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
						}
					case .None:
					}
					continue
				}
			}

			// (c) Terminal Canvas Hit: Adjust Y by -TAB_BAR_HEIGHT
			p_canvas := ev.pointer
			p_canvas.y = max(0, p_canvas.y - ui.TAB_BAR_HEIGHT)
			if !_app_route_pointer(a, p_canvas) {
				ok = false
			}
		}
	}
	if key_count > 0 {
		kitty_flags := termgrid.terminal_kitty_active(&active_b.terminal).flags
		ok = input.input_pump_events(&active_b.pty, key_evs[:key_count], kitty_flags, active_b.terminal.app_cursor_keys) && ok
	}
	quit = a.should_quit || !a.window.is_open
	return quit, ok
}

	if theme_changed {
		backend_apply_theme(&a.backend, theme)
		frontend_apply_theme(&a.frontend, theme)
	}

	if new_cfg.font_size > 0 && new_cfg.font_size != a.config.font_size {
		a.zoom_target_logical_size = new_cfg.font_size
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
	}

	config.config_destroy(&a.config)
	a.config = new_cfg
	return true
}

app_dispatch_input_events :: proc(a: ^App, evs: []input.Input_Event) -> (quit: bool, ok: bool) {
	if a == nil {
		return true, false
	}
	key_evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
	key_count := 0
	ok = true
	for ev in evs {
		switch ev.event_type {
		case .Key:
			state_before := a.backend.interaction
			consumed, action := inter.interaction_dispatch_key(&a.backend.interaction, ev, a.terminal.is_alt_screen)
			if consumed {
				if a.backend.interaction.search_active {
					q := string(a.backend.interaction.search_query[:a.backend.interaction.search_len])
					a.backend.interaction.search_match_count = inter.interaction_search_scan(
						&a.terminal,
						q,
						a.backend.interaction.search_matches[:],
					)
					if a.backend.interaction.search_match_idx >= a.backend.interaction.search_match_count {
						a.backend.interaction.search_match_idx = 0
					}
				}
				switch action {
				case .Copy:
					text := inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&a.terminal, &a.backend.interaction)
					if len(text) == 0 && state_before.selection_active {
						text = inter.interaction_extract_selection_text(&a.terminal, &state_before)
					}
					if len(text) > 0 {
						_app_clipboard_cb(transmute([]u8)text)
						delete(text)
					}
					termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&a.view, &a.terminal, 0)
					a.view_generation += 1
				case .Resume_Live:
					termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&a.view, &a.terminal, 0)
					a.view_generation += 1
				case .Scroll_To_Match:
					if a.backend.interaction.search_active && a.backend.interaction.search_match_count > 0 {
						match_row := a.backend.interaction.search_matches[a.backend.interaction.search_match_idx].row
						sb_len := termgrid.scrollback_len(&a.terminal.scrollback)
						target_offset := max(0, sb_len - match_row)
						termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&a.view, &a.terminal, target_offset)
						a.view_generation += 1
					}
				case .Paste:
					buf: [4096]u8
					n := _app_clipboard_read_cb(&a.backend, buf[:])
					if n > 0 {
						backend_paste(&a.backend, string(buf[:n]))
					}
				case .None, .Open_Link:
				}
				a.view.selection.active = a.backend.interaction.selection_active
				a.view.selection.anchor = a.backend.interaction.selection_anchor
				a.view.selection.focus = a.backend.interaction.visual_cursor
				a.view.selection.block = (a.backend.interaction.visual_kind == .Block)
				a.view.visual_mode = (a.backend.interaction.mode == .Visual)
				a.view_generation += 1
				continue
			}

			if a.pty.state == .Exited {
				switch app_handle_exited_key(ev) {
				case .Relaunch:
					_ = app_relaunch(a)
				case .Quit:
					a.should_quit = true
				case .None:
				}
			} else if key_count < len(key_evs) {
				key_evs[key_count] = ev
				key_count += 1
			}
		case .Local:
			switch ev.action {
			case .Copy:
				if !_app_copy_selection(a) {
					ok = false
				}
			case .Paste:
				if !_app_paste_clipboard(a) {
					ok = false
				}
			case .Zoom_In:
				_ = _app_request_zoom(a, 1)
			case .Zoom_Out:
				_ = _app_request_zoom(a, -1)
			case .Reload_Config:
				_ = app_reload_config(a)
			case .None:
			}
		case .Pointer:
			if !_app_route_pointer(a, ev.pointer) {
				ok = false
			}
		}
	}
	if key_count > 0 {
		kitty_flags := termgrid.terminal_kitty_active(&a.terminal).flags
		ok = input.input_pump_events(&a.pty, key_evs[:key_count], kitty_flags, a.terminal.app_cursor_keys) && ok
	}
	quit = a.should_quit || !a.window.is_open
	return quit, ok
}

Update main event watch, title sync, and frame loop for multi-session and UI staging

_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
				active_b := app_active_backend(a)
				if active_b != nil && backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
					backend_lock_render(active_b)
					state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
					_ = render.renderer_frame(&a.renderer, state.terminal, state.view)
					backend_unlock_render(active_b)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

_app_compute_hud_title :: proc(s: ^inter.Interaction_State) -> string {
	if s == nil do return ""
	switch s.mode {
	case .Visual:
		kind_str := "CHAR"
		switch s.visual_kind {
		case .Char:  kind_str = "CHAR"
		case .Line:  kind_str = "LINE"
		case .Block: kind_str = "BLOCK"
		}
		if s.paused_lines_accumulated > 0 {
			return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_VISUAL_PAUSED_FMT, kind_str, s.paused_lines_accumulated)
		}
		return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_VISUAL_FMT, kind_str)

	case .Search:
		if s.search_len == 0 {
			return APP_HUD_FIND_PROMPT
		}
		q := string(s.search_query[:s.search_len])
		if s.search_match_count == 0 {
			return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_FIND_EMPTY_FMT, q)
		}
		idx := s.search_match_idx + 1
		return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_FIND_FMT, q, idx, s.search_match_count)

	case .Passthrough:
		if s.viewport_flow == .Paused {
			if s.paused_lines_accumulated > 0 {
				return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_PAUSED_ACC_FMT, s.paused_lines_accumulated)
			}
			return APP_HUD_PAUSED_FMT
		}
	}
	return ""
}

_app_sync_title :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil do return

	if osc_title := backend_take_title(active_b); len(osc_title) > 0 {
		a.base_title = osc_title
		if !a.hud_active {
			frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, osc_title)
		}
	}

	inter_ptr := &active_b.front_interaction if backend_is_threaded(active_b) else &active_b.interaction
	if inter_ptr.mode != .Passthrough || inter_ptr.viewport_flow == .Paused {
		hud_title := _app_compute_hud_title(inter_ptr)
		if len(hud_title) > 0 {
			frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, hud_title)
			a.hud_active = true
		}
	} else if a.hud_active {
		title := a.base_title
		if len(title) == 0 {
			title = a.config.title if len(a.config.title) > 0 else APP_TITLE
		}
		frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, title)
		a.hud_active = false
	}
}

app_compute_hud_title :: _app_compute_hud_title
app_sync_title        :: _app_sync_title

_app_drain_terminal_events :: proc(a: ^App, term_ref: ^termgrid.Terminal) {
	if a == nil || term_ref == nil do return
	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil do return

	if clip_text, ok := backend_take_pending_clipboard(active_b); ok {
		win.window_set_clipboard_text(&a.window, clip_text)
	}

	if term_ref.bell_event {
		term_ref.bell_event = false
		when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
			NSBeep()
		}
	}

	for term_ref.notification_count > 0 {
		notif, ok := termgrid.terminal_pop_notification(term_ref)
		if ok {
			title := string(notif.title[:notif.title_len])
			message := string(notif.message[:notif.message_len])
			_app_show_notification(title, message)
		}
	}
}

// app_frame executes one tick of the main loop.
// Unlock_Ctx ensures swap_mutex is unlocked exactly once
Unlock_Ctx :: struct {
	backend: ^Backend,
	unlocked: bool,
}

app_frame :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}

	// (1) Drain and poll all active sessions (fair scheduling 64KB/tick)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
		_ = session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536)
	}

	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil {
		return false
	}

	// (2) Layout & Stage UI Chrome
	if a.window.handle != nil {
		tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
		_ = ui.tab_bar_layout(&a.tab_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w), tab_count, a.tab_rects[:tab_count])
		if a.search_bar.visible {
			ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w))
		}
		ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]ui.UI_Tab_Info
		for i in 0 ..< tab_count {
			t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			ui_tabs[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
				title     = string(t.title_buf[:t.title_len]),
				is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
				is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
				has_bell  = t.has_bell,
			}
		}
		_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
			&a.renderer,
			&a.ui_theme,
			a.ui_style,
			&a.tab_bar,
			ui_tabs[:tab_count],
			a.session_mgr.active_idx,
			a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
			&a.search_bar,
			f32(a.window.pixel_w),
			f32(a.window.pixel_h),
		)
	}

	if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
		// (1) Input: SDL drain feeds the backend event queue
		if a.window.handle != nil {
			evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
			n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
			quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
			if quit {
				a.should_quit = true
				return false
			}

			// Focus sync
			flags := sdl3.GetWindowFlags(a.window.handle)
			focused := .INPUT_FOCUS in flags
			if focused != active_b.focused {
				active_b.focused = focused
				backend_push_event(active_b, UI_Event{type = .Focus, focused = focused})
			}

			// Resize sync
			if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
				_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		}

		if a.should_quit || active_b.should_quit || !a.window.is_open {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}

		// (2) Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
		_app_sync_title(a)

		// (3) Render presentation of the latest Front Buffer
		backend_lock_render(active_b)
		_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.front_terminal)

		state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
		state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames

		if state.terminal != nil && (i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count) != a.renderer.rows || i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count) != a.renderer.cols) {
			render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, state.terminal, i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count), i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count))
		}
		
		ctx := Unlock_Ctx{ backend = active_b, unlocked = false }
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = proc(data: rawptr) {
			c := (^Unlock_Ctx)(data)
			if !c.unlocked {
				backend_unlock_render(c.backend)
				c.unlocked = true
			}
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = &ctx

		_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
		
		if !ctx.unlocked {
			backend_unlock_render(active_b)
			ctx.unlocked = true
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = nil
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = nil
		
		return !a.should_quit && !active_b.should_quit
	}

	// Synchronous fallback mode (used by unit tests without worker thread)
	if a.window.handle != nil {
		evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
		n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
		quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
		if quit {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
			_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		}
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
	}

	backend_drain_pty(active_b)
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.terminal)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(active_b)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
	state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
	_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)

	backend_poll_exit(active_b)

	return !a.should_quit
}
--- TARGET:
_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
				backend_lock_render(&a.backend)
				state := backend_get_render_state(&a.backend)
				_ = render.renderer_frame(&a.renderer, state.terminal, state.view)
				backend_unlock_render(&a.backend)
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

_app_compute_hud_title :: proc(s: ^inter.Interaction_State) -> string {
	if s == nil do return ""
	switch s.mode {
	case .Visual:
		kind_str := "CHAR"
		switch s.visual_kind {
		case .Char:  kind_str = "CHAR"
		case .Line:  kind_str = "LINE"
		case .Block: kind_str = "BLOCK"
		}
		if s.paused_lines_accumulated > 0 {
			return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_VISUAL_PAUSED_FMT, kind_str, s.paused_lines_accumulated)
		}
		return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_VISUAL_FMT, kind_str)

	case .Search:
		if s.search_len == 0 {
			return APP_HUD_FIND_PROMPT
		}
		q := string(s.search_query[:s.search_len])
		if s.search_match_count == 0 {
			return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_FIND_EMPTY_FMT, q)
		}
		idx := s.search_match_idx + 1
		return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_FIND_FMT, q, idx, s.search_match_count)

	case .Passthrough:
		if s.viewport_flow == .Paused {
			if s.paused_lines_accumulated > 0 {
				return fmt.tprintf(APP_HUD_PAUSED_ACC_FMT, s.paused_lines_accumulated)
			}
			return APP_HUD_PAUSED_FMT
		}
	}
	return ""
}

_app_sync_title :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return

	if osc_title := backend_take_title(&a.backend); len(osc_title) > 0 {
		a.base_title = osc_title
		if !a.hud_active {
			frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, osc_title)
		}
	}

	inter_ptr := &a.backend.front_interaction if backend_is_threaded(&a.backend) else &a.backend.interaction
	if inter_ptr.mode != .Passthrough || inter_ptr.viewport_flow == .Paused {
		hud_title := _app_compute_hud_title(inter_ptr)
		if len(hud_title) > 0 {
			frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, hud_title)
			a.hud_active = true
		}
	} else if a.hud_active {
		title := a.base_title
		if len(title) == 0 {
			title = a.config.title if len(a.config.title) > 0 else APP_TITLE
		}
		frontend_set_title(&a.frontend, title)
		a.hud_active = false
	}
}

app_compute_hud_title :: _app_compute_hud_title
app_sync_title        :: _app_sync_title

_app_drain_terminal_events :: proc(a: ^App, term_ref: ^termgrid.Terminal) {
	if a == nil || term_ref == nil do return

	if clip_text, ok := backend_take_pending_clipboard(&a.backend); ok {
		win.window_set_clipboard_text(&a.window, clip_text)
	}

	if term_ref.bell_event {
		term_ref.bell_event = false
		when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
			NSBeep()
		}
	}

	for term_ref.notification_count > 0 {
		notif, ok := termgrid.terminal_pop_notification(term_ref)
		if ok {
			title := string(notif.title[:notif.title_len])
			message := string(notif.message[:notif.message_len])
			_app_show_notification(title, message)
		}
	}
}

// app_frame executes one tick of the main loop.
// Unlock_Ctx ensures swap_mutex is unlocked exactly once
Unlock_Ctx :: struct {
	backend: ^Backend,
	unlocked: bool,
}

app_frame :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}

	if backend_is_threaded(&a.backend) {
		// (1) Input: SDL drain feeds the backend event queue
		if a.window.handle != nil {
			evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
			n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
			for i in 0..<n {
				ev := evs[i]
				switch ev.event_type {
				case .Key:
					backend_push_event(&a.backend, UI_Event{type = .Input, input = ev})
				case .Pointer:
					pt := _app_pointer_cell(a, ev.pointer.x, ev.pointer.y)
					backend_push_event(&a.backend, UI_Event{
						type = .Input,
						input = ev,
						rows = pt.row,
						cols = pt.col,
					})
					if ev.pointer.button == 1 {
						if ev.pointer.kind == .Button_Down {
							_ = win.window_capture_mouse(&a.window, true)
						} else if ev.pointer.kind == .Button_Up {
							_ = win.window_capture_mouse(&a.window, false)
						}
					}
				case .Local:
					#partial switch ev.action {
					case .Copy:
						_ = _app_copy_selection(a)
					case .Paste:
						_ = _app_paste_clipboard(a)
					case .Zoom_In:
						_ = _app_request_zoom(a, 1)
					case .Zoom_Out:
						_ = _app_request_zoom(a, -1)
					case .Reload_Config:
						_ = app_reload_config(a)
					case .None:
					}
				}
			}

			// Focus sync
			flags := sdl3.GetWindowFlags(a.window.handle)
			focused := .INPUT_FOCUS in flags
			if focused != a.focused {
				a.focused = focused
				backend_push_event(&a.backend, UI_Event{type = .Focus, focused = focused})
			}

			// Resize sync
			if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
				_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		}

		if a.should_quit || a.backend.should_quit || !a.window.is_open {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}

		// (2) Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
		_app_sync_title(a)

		// (3) Render presentation of the latest Front Buffer
		backend_lock_render(&a.backend)
		_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &a.backend.front_terminal)

		state := backend_get_render_state(&a.backend)
		state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames

		if state.terminal != nil && (i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count) != a.renderer.rows || i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count) != a.renderer.cols) {
			render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, state.terminal, i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count), i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count))
		}
		
		ctx := Unlock_Ctx{ backend = &a.backend, unlocked = false }
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = proc(data: rawptr) {
			c := (^Unlock_Ctx)(data)
			if !c.unlocked {
				backend_unlock_render(c.backend)
				c.unlocked = true
			}
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = &ctx

		_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
		
		if !ctx.unlocked {
			backend_unlock_render(&a.backend)
			ctx.unlocked = true
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = nil
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = nil
		
		return !a.should_quit && !a.backend.should_quit
	}

	// Synchronous fallback mode (used by unit tests without worker thread)
	if a.window.handle != nil {
		evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
		n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
		quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
		if quit {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
			_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		}
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
	}

	backend_drain_pty(&a.backend)
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &a.terminal)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(&a.backend)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&a.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&a.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	state := backend_get_render_state(&a.backend)
	state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
	_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)

	backend_poll_exit(&a.backend)

	return !a.should_quit
}

_app_apply_resize :: proc(a: ^App) {
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return
	rows := b.terminal.grid.row_count
	cols := b.terminal.grid.col_count
	if rows > 0 && cols > 0 {
		render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, &b.terminal, i32(rows), i32(cols))
	}
	if a.window.pixel_w > 0 && a.window.pixel_h > 0 {
		render.renderer_resize(&a.renderer, u32(a.window.pixel_w), u32(a.window.pixel_h))
	}
	a.last_px_w = a.window.pixel_w
	a.last_px_h = a.window.pixel_h
}

_app_mark_cursor_dirty :: proc(a: ^App, row, col: int) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	return backend_mark_cursor_dirty(b, row, col)
}

		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	return backend_relaunch(b)
}

app_on_resize :: proc(a: ^App, pixel_w: i32, pixel_h: i32) -> (resized: bool) {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false

	if backend_is_threaded(b) {
		backend_lock_render(b)
		defer backend_unlock_render(b)
		render.renderer_resize(&a.renderer, u32(pixel_w), u32(pixel_h))
		a.frontend.last_px_w = pixel_w
		a.frontend.last_px_h = pixel_h
		rows, cols := grid_dimensions_for_pixels(
			pixel_w,
			pixel_h,
			a.renderer.cell_width,
			a.renderer.cell_height,
			a.renderer.pad_x,
			a.renderer.pad_y,
		)
		backend_push_event(b, UI_Event{
			type = .Resize,
			pixel_w = pixel_w,
			pixel_h = pixel_h,
			rows = rows,
			cols = cols,
		})
		return true
	}
	return frontend_on_resize(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty, pixel_w, pixel_h)
}

			if consumed {
				switch s_action {
				case .Query_Changed:
					var_matches: [inter.MAX_SEARCH_MATCHES]inter.Search_Match
					_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, var_matches[:])
				case .Next_Match, .Prev_Match:
					if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
						if s_action == .Next_Match {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx + 1) % a.search_bar.match_count
						} else {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx - 1 + a.search_bar.match_count) % a.search_bar.match_count
						}
					}
				case .Close:
					a.search_bar.visible = false
				case .None:
				}
				continue
			}
		}

					a.tab_bar.hover_close_idx = -1
					a.tab_bar.hover_new_tab = false
					a.tab_bar.hover_target = .None
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true

[8eaa533b-aa1f-467f-8e6d-59a431da34dc] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Define _app_stage_ui and invoke it right before frontend_render in threaded and sync paths
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_stage_ui :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil do return
	tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
	_ = ui.tab_bar_layout(&a.tab_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w), tab_count, a.tab_rects[:tab_count])
	if a.search_bar.visible {
		ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w))
	}
	ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]ui.UI_Tab_Info
	for i in 0 ..< tab_count {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		ui_tabs[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
			title     = string(t.title_buf[:t.title_len]),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
		&a.renderer,
		&a.ui_theme,
		a.ui_style,
		&a.tab_bar,
		ui_tabs[:tab_count],
		a.session_mgr.active_idx,
		a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
		&a.search_bar,
		f32(a.window.pixel_w),
		f32(a.window.pixel_h),
	)
}

app_frame :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}

	// (1) Drain and poll all active sessions (fair scheduling 64KB/tick)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
		_ = session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536)
	}

	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil {
		return false
	}

	if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
		// (1) Input: SDL drain feeds the backend event queue
		if a.window.handle != nil {
			evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
			n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
			quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
			if quit {
				a.should_quit = true
				return false
			}

			// Focus sync
			flags := sdl3.GetWindowFlags(a.window.handle)
			focused := .INPUT_FOCUS in flags
			if focused != active_b.focused {
				active_b.focused = focused
				backend_push_event(active_b, UI_Event{type = .Focus, focused = focused})
			}

			// Resize sync
			if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
				_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		}

		if a.should_quit || active_b.should_quit || !a.window.is_open {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}

		// (2) Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
		_app_sync_title(a)

		// (3) Render presentation of the latest Front Buffer
		backend_lock_render(active_b)
		_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.front_terminal)

		state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
		state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames

		if state.terminal != nil && (i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count) != a.renderer.rows || i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count) != a.renderer.cols) {
			render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, state.terminal, i32(state.terminal.grid.row_count), i32(state.terminal.grid.col_count))
		}
		
		ctx := Unlock_Ctx{ backend = active_b, unlocked = false }
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = proc(data: rawptr) {
			c := (^Unlock_Ctx)(data)
			if !c.unlocked {
				backend_unlock_render(c.backend)
				c.unlocked = true
			}
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = &ctx

		_app_stage_ui(a)
		_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
		
		if !ctx.unlocked {
			backend_unlock_render(active_b)
			ctx.unlocked = true
		}
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = nil
		a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = nil
		
		return !a.should_quit && !active_b.should_quit
	}

	// Synchronous fallback mode (used by unit tests without worker thread)
	if a.window.handle != nil {
		evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
		n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
		quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
		if quit {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		if a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h {
			_ = app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		}
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
	}

	backend_drain_pty(active_b)
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.terminal)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(active_b)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
	state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
	_app_stage_ui(a)
	_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)

	backend_poll_exit(active_b)

	return !a.should_quit
--- TARGET:

[8f5423ad-7fe0-4082-ab4e-43f48b106e46] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update tab hotkeys to mark full_redraw_pending and update search bar key dispatch
--- REPLACEMENT:
			if ev.rune == 't' || ev.rune == 'T' {
				shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
				shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
				rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
				cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
				new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
				if spawn_ok {
					new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
					backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
					_ = backend_start_thread(new_b)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'w' || ev.rune == 'W' {
				session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, a.session_mgr.active_idx)
				if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
					a.should_quit = true
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'f' || ev.rune == 'F' {
				a.search_bar.visible = !a.search_bar.visible
				if a.search_bar.visible {
					ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w))
					inter.interaction_enter_search(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				} else {
					inter.interaction_exit_to_passthrough(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune >= '1' && ev.rune <= '9' {
				target_tab := int(ev.rune - '1')
				session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_tab)
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			}
		}

		// (2) Search Bar Key Dispatch
		if a.search_bar.visible && ev.event_type == .Key {
			consumed, s_action := ui.search_bar_dispatch_key(&a.search_bar, ev)
			if consumed {
				switch s_action {
				case .Query_Changed:
					_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, active_b.interaction.search_matches[:])
					copy(active_b.interaction.search_query[:], a.search_bar.query[:a.search_bar.query_len])
					active_b.interaction.search_len = a.search_bar.query_len
					active_b.interaction.search_match_count = a.search_bar.match_count
					active_b.interaction.search_match_idx = a.search_bar.match_idx
					active_b.interaction.search_active = true
					if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
						match_row := active_b.interaction.search_matches[active_b.interaction.search_match_idx].row
						sb_len := termgrid.scrollback_len(&active_b.terminal.scrollback)
						target_offset := clamp(sb_len - match_row, 0, termgrid.terminal_view_max_offset(&active_b.terminal))
						termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, target_offset)
						active_b.view_generation += 1
						if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
							active_b.front_view = active_b.view
						}
					}
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				case .Next_Match, .Prev_Match:
					if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
						if s_action == .Next_Match {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx + 1) % a.search_bar.match_count
						} else {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx - 1 + a.search_bar.match_count) % a.search_bar.match_count
						}
						active_b.interaction.search_match_idx = a.search_bar.match_idx
						match_row := active_b.interaction.search_matches[active_b.interaction.search_match_idx].row
						sb_len := termgrid.scrollback_len(&active_b.terminal.scrollback)
						target_offset := clamp(sb_len - match_row, 0, termgrid.terminal_view_max_offset(&active_b.terminal))
						termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, target_offset)
						active_b.view_generation += 1
						if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
							active_b.front_view = active_b.view
							active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
						}
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					}
				case .Close:
					a.search_bar.visible = false
					inter.interaction_exit_to_passthrough(&active_b.interaction)
					termgrid.terminal_view_set_offset(&active_b.view, &active_b.terminal, 0)
					active_b.view_generation += 1
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_view = active_b.view
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				case .None:
				}
				continue
			}
		}
--- TARGET:
			if ev.rune == 't' || ev.rune == 'T' {
				shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
				shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
				rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
				cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
				new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
				if spawn_ok {
					new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
					backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
					_ = backend_start_thread(new_b)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'w' || ev.rune == 'W' {
				session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, a.session_mgr.active_idx)
				if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
					a.should_quit = true
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'f' || ev.rune == 'F' {
				a.search_bar.visible = !a.search_bar.visible
				if a.search_bar.visible {
					ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, f32(a.window.pixel_w))
					inter.interaction_enter_search(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				} else {
					inter.interaction_exit_to_passthrough(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune >= '1' && ev.rune <= '9' {
				target_tab := int(ev.rune - '1')
				session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_tab)
				continue
			}
		}

		// (2) Search Bar Key Dispatch
		if a.search_bar.visible && ev.event_type == .Key {
			consumed, s_action := ui.search_bar_dispatch_key(&a.search_bar, ev)
			if consumed {
				switch s_action {
				case .Query_Changed:
					var_matches: [inter.MAX_SEARCH_MATCHES]inter.Search_Match
					_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, var_matches[:])
					copy(active_b.interaction.search_query[:], a.search_bar.query[:a.search_bar.query_len])
					active_b.interaction.search_len = a.search_bar.query_len
					active_b.interaction.search_match_count = a.search_bar.match_count
					active_b.interaction.search_match_idx = a.search_bar.match_idx
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				case .Next_Match, .Prev_Match:
					if a.search_bar.match_count > 0 {
						if s_action == .Next_Match {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx + 1) % a.search_bar.match_count
						} else {
							a.search_bar.match_idx = (a.search_bar.match_idx - 1 + a.search_bar.match_count) % a.search_bar.match_count
						}
						active_b.interaction.search_match_idx = a.search_bar.match_idx
						if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
							active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
						}
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					}
				case .Close:
					a.search_bar.visible = false
					inter.interaction_exit_to_passthrough(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				case .None:
				}
				continue
			}
		}

frontend_update_padding :: proc(f: ^Frontend) {
	if f == nil do return
	if f.window.handle == nil {
		f.renderer.pad_x = 0
		f.renderer.pad_y = 0
		return
	}
	scale := frontend_content_scale(f)
	f.renderer.pad_x = FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING * scale
	f.renderer.pad_y = (FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING + ui.TAB_BAR_HEIGHT) * scale
}

_app_stage_ui :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil do return
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
	ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]ui.UI_Tab_Info
	for i in 0 ..< tab_count {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		ui_tabs[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
			title     = string(t.title_buf[:t.title_len]),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
		&a.renderer,
		&a.ui_theme,
		a.ui_style,
		&a.tab_bar,
		ui_tabs[:tab_count],
		a.session_mgr.active_idx,
		a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
		&a.search_bar,
		f32(a.window.width) if a.window.width > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_w),
		f32(a.window.height) if a.window.height > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_h),
		frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend),
	)
	if a.confirm_dialog.visible {
		cw := a.renderer.cell_width > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_width : 8.0
		ch := a.renderer.cell_height > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_height : 16.0
		ui.ui_render_confirm_dialog(
			&a.renderer,
			&a.ui_theme,
			&a.confirm_dialog,
			f32(a.window.pixel_w),
			f32(a.window.pixel_h),
			cw,
			ch,
		)
	}
}
--- TARGET:
	if a.search_bar.visible {
		ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, window_w)
	}
}

[b3edd734-9a22-43ba-b677-078f658c4e6f] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add confirm dialog input interceptor and check tab status on Cmd+W
--- REPLACEMENT:
	for ev in evs {
		active_b = app_active_backend(a)
		if active_b == nil || a.should_quit do break

		// (0) Confirm Dialog Modal Interceptor
		if a.confirm_dialog.visible {
			if ev.event_type == .Key {
				consumed, act := ui.confirm_dialog_dispatch_key(&a.confirm_dialog, ev)
				if act == .Confirm {
					session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, a.confirm_dialog.target_tab_idx)
					ui.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
					if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					continue
				} else if act == .Cancel {
					ui.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					continue
				}
				if consumed do continue
			} else if ev.event_type == .Pointer {
				consumed, act := ui.confirm_dialog_dispatch_pointer(
					&a.confirm_dialog,
					ev.pointer.x,
					ev.pointer.y,
					ev.pointer.kind == .Button_Down,
				)
				if act == .Confirm {
					session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, a.confirm_dialog.target_tab_idx)
					ui.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
					if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					continue
				} else if act == .Cancel {
					ui.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					continue
				}
				if consumed do continue
			}
		}

		// (1) Global Hotkey Router
		if ev.event_type == .Key && ev.gui && !ev.is_release {
			if ev.rune == 't' || ev.rune == 'T' {
				shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
				shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
				rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
				cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
				new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
				if spawn_ok {
					new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
					backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'w' || ev.rune == 'W' {
				target_idx := a.session_mgr.active_idx
				if target_idx >= 0 && target_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
					target_tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[target_idx]

_app_stage_ui :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil do return
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
	ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]ui.UI_Tab_Info
	for i in 0 ..< tab_count {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		ui_tabs[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
			title     = string(t.title_buf[:t.title_len]),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
		&a.renderer,
		&a.ui_theme,
		a.ui_style,
		&a.tab_bar,
		ui_tabs[:tab_count],
		a.session_mgr.active_idx,
		a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
		&a.search_bar,
		f32(a.window.width) if a.window.width > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_w),
		f32(a.window.height) if a.window.height > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_h),
		frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend),
	)
}
--- TARGET:
	tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
	_ = ui.tab_bar_layout(&a.tab_bar, window_w, tab_count, a.tab_rects[:tab_count])
	if a.search_bar.visible {
		ui.search_bar_layout(&a.search_bar, window_w)
	}
	ui.confirm_dialog_layout(&a.confirm_dialog, f32(a.window.pixel_w), f32(a.window.pixel_h))
}

_app_stage_ui :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil do return
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	tab_count := len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
	ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]ui.UI_Tab_Info
	for i in 0 ..< tab_count {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		ui_tabs[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
			title     = string(t.title_buf[:t.title_len]),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
		&a.renderer,
		&a.ui_theme,
		a.ui_style,
		&a.tab_bar,
		ui_tabs[:tab_count],
		a.session_mgr.active_idx,
		a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
		&a.search_bar,
		f32(a.window.width) if a.window.width > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_w),
		f32(a.window.height) if a.window.height > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_h),
		frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend),
	)
	if a.confirm_dialog.visible {
		cw := a.renderer.cell_width > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_width : 8.0
		ch := a.renderer.cell_height > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_height : 16.0
		ui.ui_render_confirm_dialog(
			&a.renderer,
			&a.ui_theme,
			&a.confirm_dialog,
			f32(a.window.pixel_w),
			f32(a.window.pixel_h),
			cw,
			ch,
		)
	}
}

				} else if ev.rune == '9' {
					if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
						session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, len(a.session_mgr.tabs) - 1)
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
						continue
					}
				} else if ev.rune >= '1' && ev.rune <= '8' {
					idx := int(ev.rune - '1')
					if idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
						session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
						continue
					}
				}
			}
		}
--- TARGET:
		// (1) Global Hotkey Router
		if ev.event_type == .Key && ev.gui && !ev.is_release {
			if ev.rune == 't' || ev.rune == 'T' {
				shell, _ := _resolve_shell()
				shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
				rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
				cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
				new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
				if spawn_ok {
					new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
					backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'w' || ev.rune == 'W' {
				target_idx := a.session_mgr.active_idx
				if target_idx >= 0 && target_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
					target_tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[target_idx]
					confirmed := true
					if pty.pty_has_running_processes(&target_tab.backend.pty) {
						tab_title := string(target_tab.title_buf[:target_tab.title_len])
						confirmed = win.window_show_close_tab_alert(&a.window, tab_title)
					}
					if confirmed {
						session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, target_idx)
						if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
							a.should_quit = true
						}
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
						continue
					}
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.shift && (ev.rune == '[' || ev.rune == '{') {
				if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
					prev_idx := (a.session_mgr.active_idx - 1 + len(a.session_mgr.tabs)) % len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, prev_idx)
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.shift && (ev.rune == ']' || ev.rune == '}') {
				if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
					next_idx := (a.session_mgr.active_idx + 1) % len(a.session_mgr.tabs)
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, next_idx)
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				}
				continue
			} else if ev.rune == 'f' || ev.rune == 'F' {
				a.search_bar.visible = !a.search_bar.visible
				if a.search_bar.visible {
					_app_layout_ui(a)
					inter.interaction_enter_search(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				} else {
					inter.interaction_exit_to_passthrough(&active_b.interaction)
					if backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
						active_b.front_interaction = active_b.interaction
					}
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			} else if ev.rune >= '1' && ev.rune <= '9' {
				idx := int(ev.rune - '1')
				if idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
					session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
					a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					continue
				}
			}
		}
====================================================

[260a4b38-4eb4-48e1-ac01-92459eb104c3] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/sec/main.odin
Create main.odin orchestrating the security testing pipeline
package main

import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import "cli"
import "checks"
import "curlparse"
import "evaluate"
import "http"
import "model"
import "normalize"
import "report"
import "tokens"

read_stdin_to_string :: proc(allocator := context.allocator) -> string {
	buf := make([dynamic]u8, allocator)
	chunk: [4096]u8
	for {
		n, err := os.read(os.stdin, chunk[:])
		if n > 0 {
			append(&buf, ..chunk[:n])
		}
		if err != 0 || n == 0 {
			break
		}
	}
	return string(buf[:])
}

main :: proc() {
	config, ok := cli.parse_cli_args(os.args[1:])
	if !ok {
		os.exit(1)
	}
	if config.help {
		cli.print_usage(os.args[0])
		os.exit(0)
	}

	// 1. Read input from stdin
	raw_input := read_stdin_to_string(context.temp_allocator)
	trimmed_input := strings.trim_space(raw_input)
	if trimmed_input == "" {
		fmt.eprintln("Error: No input provided on stdin.")
		fmt.eprintln("Pipe or paste cURL commands into sec, e.g.:")
		fmt.eprintln("  sec < requests.txt")
		fmt.eprintln("  pbpaste | sec")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	// 2. Load token registry
	actors, tok_info, tok_err := tokens.resolve_and_load_tokens(config.tokens_path)
	if tok_err != "" {
		fmt.eprintfln("Token Registry Error: %s", tok_err)
		os.exit(1)
	}
	if tok_info != "" && config.verbose {
		fmt.println(tok_info)
	}

	// 3. Segment and parse cURL commands
	curl_commands := curlparse.split_into_curl_commands(trimmed_input, context.temp_allocator)
	if len(curl_commands) == 0 {
		fmt.eprintln("Error: Could not find any valid cURL commands in input.")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	parsed_requests := make([dynamic]model.Request, context.temp_allocator)

	for cmd_str, cmd_idx in curl_commands {
		cmd_tokens, tok_parse_err := curlparse.tokenize_curl_string(cmd_str, context.temp_allocator)
		if tok_parse_err != "" {
			fmt.eprintfln("\x1b[33mWarning:\x1b[0m Skipping command #%d: %s", cmd_idx+1, tok_parse_err)
			continue
		}

		req, parse_err := curlparse.parse_curl_tokens(cmd_tokens[:], cmd_idx+1)
		if parse_err != "" {
			fmt.eprintfln("\x1b[33mWarning:\x1b[0m Skipping command #%d: %s", cmd_idx+1, parse_err)
			continue
		}

		append(&parsed_requests, req)
	}

	if len(parsed_requests) == 0 {
		fmt.eprintln("Error: No valid HTTP requests parsed from cURL input.")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	// 4. Normalize paths and group into endpoint families
	families := normalize.group_endpoints(parsed_requests[:])

	// 5. Handle --dry-run mode
	if config.dry_run {
		fmt.println("\x1b[1;36m[DRY-RUN MODE]\x1b[0m Parsed endpoint families and planned mutations (no HTTP requests sent):")
		for f in families {
			fmt.printf("\nEndpoint #%d: %v %s (source: %s)\n", f.id, f.method, f.normalized_path, f.raw_path)
			fmt.println("  Detected Dynamic Parameters:")
			if len(f.params) == 0 {
				fmt.println("    (None - static endpoint)")
			} else {
				for p in f.params {
					fmt.printf("    • Segment [%d] '%s': baseline '%s' (Type: %v)\n",
						p.segment_index, p.name, p.baseline_value, p.param_type)
					fmt.printf("      Planned mutations (%d): %v\n", len(p.mutated_values), p.mutated_values)
				}
			}
			fmt.println("  Planned Checks:")
			fmt.println("    - Baseline Request & Health Check")
			fmt.println("    - Authentication Bypass Checks (missing, empty, invalid, malformed)")
			if len(actors) > 0 {
				fmt.printf("    - Cross-Actor Authorization / BOLA Checks (%d actor(s) configured)\n", len(actors))
			}
			if len(f.params) > 0 {
				fmt.println("    - Input Hardening & Boundary Checks")
			}
			if config.active {
				fmt.println("    - Sequential Replay Testing (N=3)")
			}
			if config.aggressive {
				fmt.println("    - Burst Concurrency Testing (N=5)")
			}
			fmt.println("    - Sensitive Information & Disclosure Leaks Scanning")
		}
		os.exit(0)
	}

	// 6. Execute baseline and security mutation checks for each family
	for &family in families {
		if config.verbose {
			fmt.printf("[EXEC] Running baseline for %v %s...\n", family.method, family.raw_path)
		}

		// Baseline validation guard
		family.baseline_resp = http.execute_request(&family.base_request)

		if family.baseline_resp.transport_error != "" {
			res := model.Check_Result{
				id       = "baseline-transport-error",
				category = .Authentication,
				status   = .ERROR,
				title    = "Baseline Request Failed",
				detail   = fmt.tprintf("Transport error: %s. Endpoint testing aborted.", family.baseline_resp.transport_error),
				baseline = model.summarize_response(&family.baseline_resp),
				observed = model.summarize_response(&family.baseline_resp),
			}
			append(&family.check_results, res)
			continue
		}

		// Baseline response status check
		if family.baseline_resp.status < 200 || family.baseline_resp.status >= 300 {
			res := model.Check_Result{
				id       = "baseline-unhealthy-status",
				category = .Authentication,
				status   = .WARN,
				title    = fmt.tprintf("Baseline Returned HTTP %d", family.baseline_resp.status),
				detail   = "Baseline request did not return a successful 2xx response. Security mutations skipped to avoid false positives.",
				baseline = model.summarize_response(&family.baseline_resp),
				observed = model.summarize_response(&family.baseline_resp),
			}
			append(&family.check_results, res)
			continue
		}

		// Baseline Disclosure Check
		base_leaks := evaluate.evaluate_disclosure(&family.baseline_resp)
		for l in base_leaks {
			append(&family.check_results, l)
		}

		// Phase 5: Authentication Checks
		auth_results := checks.run_auth_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp)
		for r in auth_results do append(&family.check_results, r)

		// Phase 6: Cross-Actor Authorization Checks
		if len(actors) > 0 {
			authz_results := checks.run_authorization_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp, actors[:])
			for r in authz_results do append(&family.check_results, r)
		}

		// Phase 7: Path Input Hardening Checks
		if len(family.params) > 0 {
			path_results := checks.run_path_input_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp, family.params[:])
			for r in path_results do append(&family.check_results, r)
		}

		// Phase 8: Body Input Checks
		body_results := checks.run_body_input_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp)
		for r in body_results do append(&family.check_results, r)

		// Phase 11: Replay Checks (--active)
		if config.active {
			replay_results := checks.run_replay_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp, 3)
			for r in replay_results do append(&family.check_results, r)
		}

		// Phase 12: Concurrency Checks (--aggressive)
		if config.aggressive {
			conc_results := checks.run_concurrency_checks(&family.base_request, &family.baseline_resp, 5)
			for r in conc_results do append(&family.check_results, r)
		}
	}

	// 7. Render Terminal Checklist Report
	report.render_terminal_report(families[:], config.verbose)

	// 8. Export JSON report if requested
	if config.json_path != "" {
		json_err := report.write_json_report(config.json_path, families[:])
		if json_err != "" {
			fmt.eprintfln("\x1b[31mError exporting JSON report:\x1b[0m %s", json_err)
		} else {
			fmt.printf("\n\x1b[32m✔ JSON report saved to %s\x1b[0m\n", config.json_path)
		}
	}

	// 9. Determine exit code
	has_fail := false
	for f in families {
		for r in f.check_results {
			if r.status == .FAIL {
				has_fail = true
				break
			}
		}
		if has_fail do break
	}

	if has_fail {
		os.exit(1)
	} else {
		os.exit(0)
	}
}

[ae15ba43-a454-4a33-9569-20ea76036dac] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Replace _app_event_watch with new implementation that invokes _app_stage_ui and frontend_render on resize and exposed events
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			if size_changed || event.type == .WINDOW_EXPOSED {
				active_b := app_active_backend(a)
				if active_b != nil {
					threaded := backend_is_threaded(active_b)
					if threaded {
						backend_lock_render(active_b)
					}
					state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
					state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
					_app_stage_ui(a)
					_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
					if threaded {
						backend_unlock_render(active_b)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

	}

			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)

					backend_unlock_render(active_b)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return true

// frontend_flush_pending_pty_resize flushes any pending debounced PTY size update.
frontend_flush_pending_pty_resize :: proc(f: ^Frontend, pty_ptr: ^pty.Pty) -> bool {
	if f == nil || !f.pty_resize_pending || pty_ptr == nil {
		return false
	}
	pty.pty_set_winsize(pty_ptr, f.pending_pty_rows, f.pending_pty_cols)
	f.last_pty_resize_ms = sdl3.GetTicks()
	f.pty_resize_pending = false
	return true
}

[a2376e27-6a3e-4e5b-bb43-2d7fedd13878] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update app_on_resize for multi-tab sync and _app_event_watch for leak prevention and active pty drain
--- REPLACEMENT:
app_on_resize :: proc(a: ^App, pixel_w: i32, pixel_h: i32) -> (resized: bool) {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false

	frontend_update_padding(&a.frontend)

	rows, cols := grid_dimensions_for_pixels(
		pixel_w,
		pixel_h,
		a.renderer.cell_width,
		a.renderer.cell_height,
		a.renderer.pad_x,
		a.renderer.pad_y,
	)

	if backend_is_threaded(b) {
		backend_lock_render(b)
		defer backend_unlock_render(b)
		render.renderer_resize(&a.renderer, u32(pixel_w), u32(pixel_h))
		a.frontend.last_px_w = pixel_w
		a.frontend.last_px_h = pixel_h
		backend_push_event(b, UI_Event{
			type = .Resize,
			pixel_w = pixel_w,
			pixel_h = pixel_h,
			rows = rows,
			cols = cols,
		})
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if &tab.backend != b && (tab.backend.terminal.grid.row_count != rows || tab.backend.terminal.grid.col_count != cols) {
				termgrid.terminal_resize(&tab.backend.terminal, rows, cols)
				termgrid.damage_mark_all(&tab.backend.terminal.damage, nil)
				tab.backend.view_generation += 1
			}
		}
		return true
	}

	resized = frontend_on_resize(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty, pixel_w, pixel_h)

	for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		if tab.backend.terminal.grid.row_count != rows || tab.backend.terminal.grid.col_count != cols {
			termgrid.terminal_resize(&tab.backend.terminal, rows, cols)
			termgrid.damage_mark_all(&tab.backend.terminal.damage, nil)
			tab.backend.view_generation += 1
		}
	}
	return resized
}

_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			active_b := app_active_backend(a)
			if active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
			}
			if size_changed || event.type == .WINDOW_EXPOSED {
				if active_b != nil {
					threaded := backend_is_threaded(active_b)
					if threaded {
						backend_lock_render(active_b)
					}
					state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
					state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
					_app_stage_ui(a)
					_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
					if threaded {
						backend_unlock_render(active_b)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

[a2376e27-6a3e-4e5b-bb43-2d7fedd13878] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add temp_allocator cleanup and pty resize flush at start of app_frame
--- REPLACEMENT:
app_frame :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b != nil {
		frontend_flush_pending_pty_resize(&a.frontend, &active_b.pty)
	}

	// (1) Drain and poll all active sessions (fair scheduling 64KB/tick)
	had_tabs := len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0
	if had_tabs {
		_ = session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536)
		if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
	}

	if a.should_quit {
		return false
	}

	active_b = app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil {
		return false
	}
--- TARGET:

		}
	}

	if a.should_quit {
		return false
	}

	if active_b == nil {
		return false
