[04b34078-e3e4-4c1f-b0cf-bfeb78f6c566] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add monitor fields to App struct and define pty_monitor_proc
--- REPLACEMENT:
App :: struct {
	session_mgr:         Session_Manager,
	using backend:       Backend, // Active tab backend fallback for tests/headless
	using frontend:      Frontend,
	tab_bar:             ui.Tab_Bar_State,
	tab_rects:           [MAX_TABS]ui.Rect_f32,
	search_bar:          ui.Search_Bar_State,
	tab_drag:            ui.Tab_Drag_State,
	tab_menu:            ui.Tab_Menu_State,
	tab_rename:          ui.Tab_Rename_State,
	tab_overflow:        ui.Tab_Overflow_State,
	ui_theme:            ui.UI_Theme,
	base_title:          string,
	hud_active:          bool,
	confirm_dialog:      ui.Confirm_Dialog_State,
	drag_region:         win.Drag_Region,
	pty_monitor_thread:  ^thread.Thread,
	pty_monitor_running: b32,
	pty_event_pending:   b32,
	pending_wake_event:  sdl3.Event,
	has_wake_event:      bool,
}

pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [MAX_TABS + 1]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
				if count < len(pfds) {
					pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
					count += 1
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}

		if count == 0 {
			time.sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
			continue
		}

		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 50)
		if r > 0 {
			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				}
				time.sleep(1 * time.Millisecond)
			}
		}
	}
}

--- TARGET:
App :: struct {
	session_mgr:    Session_Manager,

[04b34078-e3e4-4c1f-b0cf-bfeb78f6c566] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Start monitor thread in app_init and stop it in app_destroy
--- REPLACEMENT:
	_ = sdl3.AddEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)

	sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_monitor_running, true)
	sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, false)
	a.pty_monitor_thread = thread.create(pty_monitor_proc)
	if a.pty_monitor_thread != nil {
		a.pty_monitor_thread.data = a
		thread.start(a.pty_monitor_thread)
	}

	return true
}

// app_destroy tears down Session Manager and Frontend in order.
app_destroy :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil {
		return
	}
	if a.pty_monitor_thread != nil {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_monitor_running, false)
		thread.join(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		thread.destroy(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		a.pty_monitor_thread = nil
	}
	sdl3.RemoveEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)
	session_manager_destroy(&a.session_mgr)
	if a.backend.drain_buf != nil {
		backend_destroy(&a.backend)
	}
	_ = win.window_clear_drag_region(&a.window)
	frontend_destroy(&a.frontend)
	config.config_destroy(&a.config)
}
--- TARGET:
	_ = sdl3.AddEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)
	return true
}

// app_destroy tears down Session Manager and Frontend in order.
app_destroy :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil {
		return
	}
	sdl3.RemoveEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)

		backend_destroy(&a.backend)

			_ = win.window_zoom(&a.window)
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		}
		active_b = app_active_backend(a)
		if active_b == nil do return false
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		resized := app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		if resized && len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
			if session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536) {
				any_damaged = true
			}
		}
	}

	// Legacy headless callers do not use the session manager.
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
		if backend_drain_pty(active_b) > 0 {
			any_damaged = true
		}
	}
	if _damage_cells(&active_b.terminal) > 0 {
		any_damaged = true
	}
	if a.renderer.rows != i32(active_b.terminal.grid.row_count) || a.renderer.cols != i32(active_b.terminal.grid.col_count) {
		render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, &active_b.terminal, i32(active_b.terminal.grid.row_count), i32(active_b.terminal.grid.col_count))
		any_damaged = true
	}
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.terminal)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(active_b)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
	state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
	anim_active := a.tab_bar.anim.anim_active
	if ui.tab_bar_anim_update(&a.tab_bar, a.session_mgr.active_idx, _app_frame_dt_ms(), a.ui_theme.motion.hover_ms, a.ui_theme.motion.active_ms) {
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		anim_active = true
	}

	is_dirty := any_damaged || n_events > 0 || a.renderer.full_redraw_pending || anim_active
	if is_dirty {
		_app_stage_ui(a)
		_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
	}

	backend_poll_exit(active_b)

	return !a.should_quit
}

main :: proc() {
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	fmt.printf("Term: starting %s (%dx%d)\n", shell, APP_DEFAULT_COLS, APP_DEFAULT_ROWS)

	app := new(App, runtime.heap_allocator())
	if !app_init(app, APP_DEFAULT_ROWS, APP_DEFAULT_COLS, shell, shell_argv) {
		free(app, runtime.heap_allocator())
		fmt.println("ERROR: app_init failed")
		return
	}
	defer {
		app_destroy(app)
		free(app, runtime.heap_allocator())
	}

	for !app.should_quit {
		if !app_frame(app) {
			break
		}
		if app.should_quit {
			break
		}

		anim_active := app.tab_bar.anim.anim_active
		ev: sdl3.Event
		has_ev := false
		if anim_active {
			if sdl3.WaitEventTimeout(&ev, 16) {
				has_ev = true
			}
		} else {
			if sdl3.WaitEvent(&ev) {
				has_ev = true
			}
		}

		if has_ev {
			app.pending_wake_event = ev
			app.has_wake_event = true
		}
	}
	fmt.println("Term: quit")
}
--- TARGET:
	// (1) Drain and poll all active sessions (fair scheduling 64KB/tick)
	had_tabs := len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0
	if had_tabs {
		_ = session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536)
		if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
	}

	if a.should_quit {
		return false
	}

	active_b := app_active_backend(a)
	if active_b == nil {
		return false
	}

	// The main loop owns PTY parsing and terminal state for every tab.
	if a.window.handle != nil {
		evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
		n := input.window_poll_input(&a.window, evs[:], input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS)
		quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n])
		if quit {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}
		if win.window_take_titlebar_double_click(&a.drag_region) {
			_ = win.window_zoom(&a.window)
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		}
		active_b = app_active_backend(a)
		if active_b == nil do return false
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		resized := app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		if resized && len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
			_ = session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536)
		}
	}

	// Legacy headless callers do not use the session manager.
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 {
		backend_drain_pty(active_b)
	}
	if a.renderer.rows != i32(active_b.terminal.grid.row_count) || a.renderer.cols != i32(active_b.terminal.grid.col_count) {
		render.renderer_resize_grid(&a.renderer, &active_b.terminal, i32(active_b.terminal.grid.row_count), i32(active_b.terminal.grid.col_count))
	}
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, &active_b.terminal)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(active_b)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(&active_b.terminal)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
	state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
	if ui.tab_bar_anim_update(&a.tab_bar, a.session_mgr.active_idx, _app_frame_dt_ms(), a.ui_theme.motion.hover_ms, a.ui_theme.motion.active_ms) {
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
	}
	_app_stage_ui(a)
	_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)

	backend_poll_exit(active_b)

	return !a.should_quit
}

main :: proc() {
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }

// app_destroy tears down Session Manager and Frontend in order.
app_destroy :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil {
		return
	}
	if a.pty_monitor_thread != nil {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_monitor_running, false)
		odin_thread.join(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		odin_thread.destroy(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		a.pty_monitor_thread = nil
	}

	}

}

_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			active_b := app_active_backend(a)
			if active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
			}
--- TARGET:
		a.last_px_h = pixel_h
		backend_push_event(b, UI_Event{
			type = .Resize,
			pixel_w = pixel_w,
			pixel_h = pixel_h,
			rows = rows,
			cols = cols,
		})
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if &tab.backend != b && (tab.backend.terminal.grid.row_count != rows || tab.backend.terminal.grid.col_count != cols) {
				termgrid.terminal_resize(&tab.backend.terminal, rows, cols)
				termgrid.damage_mark_all(&tab.backend.terminal.damage, nil)
				tab.backend.view_generation += 1
			}
		}
		return true
	}

	resized = frontend_on_resize(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty, pixel_w, pixel_h)
	a.last_px_w = pixel_w
	a.last_px_h = pixel_h

	for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		if &tab.backend != b && (tab.backend.terminal.grid.row_count != rows || tab.backend.terminal.grid.col_count != cols) {
			termgrid.terminal_resize(&tab.backend.terminal, rows, cols)
			pty.pty_set_winsize(&tab.backend.pty, rows, cols)
			termgrid.damage_mark_all(&tab.backend.terminal.damage, nil)
			tab.backend.view_generation += 1
		}
	}
	return resized
}

_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			new_rows, new_cols := grid_dimensions_for_pixels(
				a.window.pixel_w,
				a.window.pixel_h,
				a.renderer.cell_width,
				a.renderer.cell_height,
				a.renderer.pad_x,
				a.renderer.pad_y,
			)
			active_b := app_active_backend(a)
			cur_rows := active_b.terminal.grid.row_count if active_b != nil else 0
			cur_cols := active_b.terminal.grid.col_count if active_b != nil else 0
			grid_changed := (new_rows != cur_rows || new_cols != cur_cols)
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			if grid_changed && active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				if pty.pty_wait_readable(&active_b.pty, 4) {
					_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
				}
			} else if active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
			}

[9e7e2e72-02da-46d4-bdfa-1e2aeaacf90c] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Throttle live drag rendering in _app_event_watch using _last_drag_render_ns
--- REPLACEMENT:
_last_drag_render_ns: u64 = 0

_app_event_watch :: proc "c" (userdata: rawptr, event: ^sdl3.Event) -> bool {
	if event == nil || userdata == nil {
		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			active_b := app_active_backend(a)
			if active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
			}

			now_ns := u64(platform.platform_ticks_to_ns(platform.platform_now()))
			is_exposed := event.type == .WINDOW_EXPOSED
			time_since_last := now_ns - _last_drag_render_ns
			// Throttle live drag rendering to display refresh rate (~8ms / 120 FPS max).
			// WINDOW_EXPOSED always renders immediately.
			should_render := is_exposed || time_since_last >= 8_000_000

			if should_render && (size_changed || is_exposed) {
				_last_drag_render_ns = now_ns
				if active_b != nil {
					threaded := backend_is_threaded(active_b)
					if threaded {
						backend_lock_render(active_b)
					}
					state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
					state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
					_app_stage_ui(a)
					_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
					if threaded {
						backend_unlock_render(active_b)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}
--- TARGET:

		return true
	}
	#partial switch event.type {
	case .WINDOW_RESIZED, .WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED, .WINDOW_EXPOSED:
		context = runtime.default_context()
		defer free_all(context.temp_allocator)
		a := (^App)(userdata)
		if a.window.handle != nil && event.window.windowID == sdl3.GetWindowID(a.window.handle) {
			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			size_changed := a.window.pixel_w != a.last_px_w || a.window.pixel_h != a.last_px_h
			if size_changed {
				app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
			}
			active_b := app_active_backend(a)
			if active_b != nil && active_b.pty.master >= 0 && !backend_is_threaded(active_b) {
				_ = backend_drain_pty(active_b)
			}
			if size_changed || event.type == .WINDOW_EXPOSED {
				if active_b != nil {
					threaded := backend_is_threaded(active_b)

						backend_lock_render(active_b)

						backend_unlock_render(active_b)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

Update tab_drag_begin, tab_bar_anim_activate, tab_rename procedures to use platform_tabs

// _app_commit_tab_drop maps a drop boundary to a final index and reorders the
// session manager, then rebinds modals and arms the tab chrome animation.
_app_commit_tab_drop :: proc(a: ^App, from_idx, drop_gap: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	to_idx := drop_gap
	if to_idx > from_idx do to_idx -= 1
	_ = session_reorder_tab(&a.session_mgr, from_idx, to_idx)
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
	platform_tabs.tab_bar_anim_activate(&a.tab_bar)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_begin_tab_rename starts inline editing on a tab seeded with its display title.
_app_begin_tab_rename :: proc(a: ^App, tab_idx: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	if tab_idx < 0 || tab_idx >= len(a.session_mgr.tabs) do return
	tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[tab_idx]
	title := session_title_display(tab)
	_ = platform_tabs.tab_rename_begin(&a.tab_rename, tab_idx, tab.id, title)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_commit_tab_rename applies the edit buffer; an empty buffer clears the override.
_app_commit_tab_rename :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || !a.tab_rename.active do return
	tab_idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_rename.tab_id)
	if tab_idx >= 0 {
		text := platform_tabs.tab_rename_text(&a.tab_rename)
		if len(text) == 0 {
			_ = session_clear_title_override(&a.session_mgr.tabs[tab_idx])
		} else {
			_ = session_set_title_override(&a.session_mgr.tabs[tab_idx], text)
		}
	}
	_app_cancel_tab_rename(a)
}

// _app_cancel_tab_rename abandons the edit session.
_app_cancel_tab_rename :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	platform_tabs.tab_rename_cancel(&a.tab_rename)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}
--- TARGET:
	tab_id := a.session_mgr.tabs[tab_idx].id
	_ = ui.tab_drag_begin(&a.tab_drag, tab_idx, tab_id, px, py)
}

_app_commit_tab_drop :: proc(a: ^App, from_idx, drop_gap: int) {

	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

}

[9ece0965-19f1-4ba4-9063-8ee922056fbc] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update tab_menu, overflow, and build tabs to use platform_tabs
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_resync_tab_modals :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	if a.tab_menu.visible {
		idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_menu.target_id)
		if idx < 0 {
			platform_tabs.tab_menu_close(&a.tab_menu)
		} else {
			a.tab_menu.target_idx = idx
		}
	}
	if a.tab_rename.active {
		idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_rename.tab_id)
		if idx < 0 {
			platform_tabs.tab_rename_cancel(&a.tab_rename)
		} else {
			a.tab_rename.tab_idx = idx
		}
	}
}

// _app_build_ui_tabs snapshots tab display metadata for chrome rendering and
// overflow refresh. Titles borrow session buffers and are valid only during the call.
_app_build_ui_tabs :: proc(a: ^App, out: []platform_tabs.Tab_Info) -> int {
	if a == nil do return 0
	n := min(len(a.session_mgr.tabs), len(out))
	for i in 0 ..< n {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		out[i] = platform_tabs.Tab_Info{
			id        = t.id,
			title     = session_title_display(t),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	return n
}

// _app_open_tab_overflow toggles the all-tabs dropdown. It only opens when the
// tab bar is actually overflowing, seeding selection with the active tab.
_app_open_tab_overflow :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	if a.tab_overflow.visible {
		platform_tabs.tab_overflow_close(&a.tab_overflow)
		_app_set_drag_region(a)
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		return
	}
	if a.tab_bar.overflow_rect.w <= 0 do return
	a.tab_overflow = platform_tabs.Tab_Overflow_State{visible = true, selected_tab_id = _app_active_tab_id(a)}
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	_app_set_drag_region(a)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_activate_overflow_selection switches to the dropdown's selected tab and closes it.
_app_activate_overflow_selection :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_overflow.selected_tab_id)
	if idx >= 0 {
		_ = session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
		_ = platform_tabs.tab_bar_scroll_to_tab(&a.tab_bar, idx)
	}
	platform_tabs.tab_overflow_close(&a.tab_overflow)
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
	platform_tabs.tab_bar_anim_activate(&a.tab_bar)
	_app_set_drag_region(a)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_execute_tab_menu performs a chosen context menu action against its target tab.
_app_execute_tab_menu :: proc(a: ^App, item: platform_tabs.Tab_Menu_Item, target_idx: int) {
--- TARGET:
_app_resync_tab_modals :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	if a.tab_menu.visible {
		idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_menu.target_id)
		if idx < 0 {
			ui.tab_menu_close(&a.tab_menu)
		} else {
			a.tab_menu.target_idx = idx
		}
	}
	if a.tab_rename.active {
		idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.tab_rename.tab_id)
		if idx < 0 {
			ui.tab_rename_cancel(&a.tab_rename)
		} else {
			a.tab_rename.tab_idx = idx
		}
	}
}

// _app_build_ui_tabs snapshots tab display metadata for chrome rendering and
// overflow refresh. Titles borrow session buffers and are valid only during the call.
_app_build_ui_tabs :: proc(a: ^App, out: []ui.UI_Tab_Info) -> int {
	if a == nil do return 0
	n := min(len(a.session_mgr.tabs), len(out))
	for i in 0 ..< n {
		t := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
		out[i] = ui.UI_Tab_Info{
			id        = t.id,
			title     = session_title_display(t),
			is_active = (i == a.session_mgr.active_idx),
			is_exited = (t.status == .Exited),
			has_bell  = t.has_bell,
		}
	}
	return n
}

// _app_open_tab_overflow toggles the all-tabs dropdown. It only opens when the
// tab bar is actually overflowing, seeding selection with the active tab.
_app_open_tab_overflow :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return

		_app_set_drag_region(a)
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		return
	}
	if a.tab_bar.overflow_rect.w <= 0 do return
	a.tab_overflow = ui.Tab_Overflow_State{visible = true, selected_tab_id = _app_active_tab_id(a)}
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	_app_set_drag_region(a)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_execute_tab_menu performs a chosen context menu action against its target tab.
_app_execute_tab_menu :: proc(a: ^App, item: ui.Tab_Menu_Item, target_idx: int) {

// _app_close_tab_confirmed closes the tab at idx and rebinds the chrome modals
// that pointed at it. Closing the final tab requests application quit.
_app_close_tab_confirmed :: proc(a: ^App, idx: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(a.session_mgr.tabs) do return
	_ = session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
}

// _app_request_close_tab opens the in-app confirm dialog when the tab still has
// a running process, otherwise it closes the tab immediately.
_app_request_close_tab :: proc(a: ^App, idx: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(a.session_mgr.tabs) do return
	tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[idx]
	if pty.pty_has_running_processes(&tab.backend.pty) {
		platform_dialogs.confirm_dialog_show(&a.confirm_dialog, idx, tab.id)
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		_app_set_drag_region(a)
		return
	}
	_app_close_tab_confirmed(a, idx)
}

// _app_apply_confirm_action resolves a dialog action against its target tab,
// hides the dialog, and refreshes the drag region and a full redraw.
_app_apply_confirm_action :: proc(a: ^App, action: platform_dialogs.Confirm_Dialog_Action) {
	if a == nil do return
	switch action {
	case .Confirm:
		idx := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, a.confirm_dialog.target_tab_id)
		platform_dialogs.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
		if idx >= 0 do _app_close_tab_confirmed(a, idx)
	case .Cancel:
		platform_dialogs.confirm_dialog_hide(&a.confirm_dialog)
	case .None:
	}
--- TARGET:
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
	ui.tab_bar_anim_activate(&a.tab_bar)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

					case .Changed, .None:
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true

					continue

					ev.pointer.button,
				)
				if consumed {
					switch d_action {
					case .Drop:
						resolved := _app_tab_index_by_id(a, from_id)
						if resolved < 0 do resolved = from_idx
						_app_commit_tab_drop(a, resolved, drop_gap)
					case .Cancel, .Move, .None:
						a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
					}
					continue
				}
			}

				switch o_action {
				case .Activate:
					_app_activate_overflow_selection(a)
				case .Dismiss:
					ui.tab_overflow_close(&a.tab_overflow)
					_app_set_drag_region(a)
				case .None:
				}
				a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
				continue
			}

				if consumed {
					switch s_action {
					case .Query_Changed:
						_ = ui.search_bar_execute_scan(&a.search_bar, &active_b.terminal, active_b.interaction.search_matches[:])

[c51a4ae9-3790-4e82-b2ed-f6c1b6648b1a] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/frontend.odin
Add frontend_font_paths and update find_font
--- REPLACEMENT:
// frontend_font_paths expands candidate font paths with application resource locations.
frontend_font_paths :: proc(executable_path: string, candidates: []string, allocator := context.allocator) -> []string {
	res := make([dynamic]string, allocator)
	if len(executable_path) > 0 {
		dir := filepath.dir(executable_path, context.temp_allocator)
		parent := filepath.dir(dir, context.temp_allocator)

		// 1. Resources/fonts
		for c in candidates {
			if strings.has_prefix(c, "assets/fonts/") {
				filename := c[len("assets/fonts/"):]
				p := filepath.join({parent, "Resources/fonts", filename}, allocator)
				append(&res, p)
			}
		}

		// 2. assets/fonts
		for c in candidates {
			if strings.has_prefix(c, "assets/fonts/") {
				filename := c[len("assets/fonts/"):]
				p := filepath.join({parent, "assets/fonts", filename}, allocator)
				append(&res, p)
			}
		}
	}

	for c in candidates {
		append(&res, strings.clone(c, allocator))
	}

	return res[:]
}

frontend_font_paths_destroy :: proc(paths: []string, allocator := context.allocator) {
	for p in paths {
		delete(p, allocator)
	}
	delete(paths, allocator)
}

// find_font tries to find a usable font file.
find_font :: proc(allocator := context.allocator) -> (path: string, ok: bool) {
	for font_path in FONT_PATHS {
		actual_path := font_path
		if strings.has_prefix(font_path, "~/") {
			if home, hok := os.lookup_env("HOME", context.temp_allocator); hok {
				actual_path = strings.concatenate({home, font_path[1:]}, context.temp_allocator)
			}
		}
		if data, err := os.read_entire_file(actual_path, context.allocator); err == nil {
			delete(data)
			return strings.clone(actual_path, allocator), true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}
--- TARGET:

[58faeaa2-92dd-478a-9691-f262361ef21f] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/cmd/term_mcp/main.odin
Implement stdio transport and JSON-RPC dispatcher in main.odin
package main

import "core:encoding/json"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strconv"
import "core:strings"

global_sm: Session_Manager

main :: proc() {
	session_manager_init(&global_sm)
	defer session_manager_destroy_all(&global_sm)

	fmt.eprintfln("[term-mcp] Server starting (PID: %d)", os.getpid())

	read_buf: [4096]u8
	acc: [dynamic]u8
	defer delete(acc)

	for {
		n, err := os.read(os.stdin, read_buf[:])
		if err != nil || n <= 0 {
			break
		}

		for i in 0 ..< n {
			append(&acc, read_buf[i])
		}

		// Process all complete messages in accumulator
		for len(acc) > 0 {
			acc_str := string(acc[:])

			// 1. Check for Content-Length framing (LSP / MCP standard variant)
			if strings.has_prefix(acc_str, "Content-Length:") {
				header_end := strings.index(acc_str, "\r\n\r\n")
				sep_len := 4
				if header_end == -1 {
					header_end = strings.index(acc_str, "\n\n")
					sep_len = 2
				}

				if header_end != -1 {
					header_line := acc_str[len("Content-Length:") : header_end]
					content_len := strconv.atoi(strings.trim_space(header_line))
					body_start := header_end + sep_len
					if len(acc) >= body_start + content_len {
						body := string(acc[body_start : body_start + content_len])
						dispatch_message(body)

						// Shift accumulator past consumed message
						total_consumed := body_start + content_len
						copy(acc[:], acc[total_consumed:])
						resize(&acc, len(acc) - total_consumed)
						continue
					}
				}
				// Incomplete Content-Length payload: wait for more bytes
				break
			}

			// 2. Check for newline-delimited JSON (standard MCP stdio)
			newline_idx := strings.index_byte(acc_str, '\n')
			if newline_idx != -1 {
				line := strings.trim_space(acc_str[:newline_idx])
				total_consumed := newline_idx + 1

				copy(acc[:], acc[total_consumed:])
				resize(&acc, len(acc) - total_consumed)

				if len(line) > 0 {
					dispatch_message(line)
				}
				continue
			}

			// Incomplete line: wait for more bytes
			break
		}
	}

	fmt.eprintfln("[term-mcp] Shutting down cleanly")
}

// dispatch_message handles a single JSON-RPC message string.
dispatch_message :: proc(raw_json: string) {
	req, raw_val, ok, err_code, err_msg := parse_json_rpc_request(raw_json, context.temp_allocator)
	defer {
		if raw_val != nil {
			json.destroy_value(raw_val)
		}
	}

	if !ok {
		// Only respond with error if not a notification
		resp := make_json_rpc_error(nil, err_code, err_msg, context.temp_allocator)
		send_response(resp)
		return
	}

	// Notifications have no id and must not receive a response
	if req.is_notification {
		#partial switch req.method {
		case "notifications/initialized", "initialized":
			// Handshake acknowledgment notification: no-op
		case:
			fmt.eprintfln("[term-mcp] Notification ignored: %s", req.method)
		}
		return
	}

	switch req.method {
	case "initialize":
		// Handle MCP initialize handshake
		result := fmt.tprintf(
			`{"protocolVersion":"%s","capabilities":{"tools":{}},"serverInfo":{"name":"%s","version":"%s"}}`,
			MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
			MCP_SERVER_NAME,
			MCP_SERVER_VERSION,
		)
		resp := make_json_rpc_result(req.id, result, context.temp_allocator)
		send_response(resp)

	case "ping":
		resp := make_json_rpc_result(req.id, "{}", context.temp_allocator)
		send_response(resp)

	case "tools/list":
		resp := make_json_rpc_result(req.id, TOOLS_LIST_JSON, context.temp_allocator)
		send_response(resp)

	case "tools/call":
		params_obj, is_obj := req.params.(json.Object)
		if !is_obj {
			resp := make_json_rpc_error(req.id, RPC_INVALID_PARAMS, "params must be an object", context.temp_allocator)
			send_response(resp)
			return
		}

		tool_name := _json_get_string(params_obj, "name", "")
		if len(tool_name) == 0 {
			resp := make_json_rpc_error(req.id, RPC_INVALID_PARAMS, "Missing tool 'name'", context.temp_allocator)
			send_response(resp)
			return
		}

		args_val := params_obj["arguments"]
		result_json, is_err, err_detail := tools_dispatch_call(&global_sm, tool_name, args_val, context.temp_allocator)
		if is_err {
			resp := make_json_rpc_error(req.id, RPC_INVALID_PARAMS, err_detail, context.temp_allocator)
			send_response(resp)
		} else {
			resp := make_json_rpc_result(req.id, result_json, context.temp_allocator)
			send_response(resp)
		}

	case:
		resp := make_json_rpc_error(req.id, RPC_METHOD_NOT_FOUND, fmt.tprintf("Method not found: '%s'", req.method), context.temp_allocator)
		send_response(resp)
	}
}

// send_response writes a JSON-RPC response to stdout followed by a newline and flushes.
send_response :: proc(resp: string) {
	os.write_string(os.stdout, resp)
	os.write_string(os.stdout, "\n")
	os.flush(os.stdout)
}

[1787a119-f8ab-4946-ba06-70d2ee243d93] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/frontend.odin
Update find_font to accept executable_path with fallback to os.get_executable_path and os.args[0], expanding candidates via frontend_font_paths
--- REPLACEMENT:
// find_font tries to find a usable font file.
find_font :: proc(executable_path: string = "", allocator := context.allocator) -> (path: string, ok: bool) {
	exec := executable_path
	if len(exec) == 0 {
		if p, err := os.get_executable_path(context.temp_allocator); err == nil && len(p) > 0 {
			exec = p
		} else if len(os.args) > 0 && len(os.args[0]) > 0 {
			exec = os.args[0]
		}
	}

	candidates := frontend_font_paths(exec, FONT_PATHS, context.temp_allocator)
	defer frontend_font_paths_destroy(candidates, context.temp_allocator)

	for font_path in candidates {
		actual_path := font_path
		if strings.has_prefix(font_path, "~/") {
			if home, hok := os.lookup_env("HOME", context.temp_allocator); hok {
				actual_path = strings.concatenate({home, font_path[1:]}, context.temp_allocator)
			}
		}
		if data, err := os.read_entire_file(actual_path, context.allocator); err == nil {
			delete(data)
			return strings.clone(actual_path, allocator), true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}
--- TARGET:
// find_font tries to find a usable font file.
find_font :: proc(allocator := context.allocator) -> (path: string, ok: bool) {
	for font_path in FONT_PATHS {
		actual_path := font_path
		if strings.has_prefix(font_path, "~/") {
			if home, hok := os.lookup_env("HOME", context.temp_allocator); hok {
				actual_path = strings.concatenate({home, font_path[1:]}, context.temp_allocator)
			}
		}
		if data, err := os.read_entire_file(actual_path, context.allocator); err == nil {
			delete(data)
			return strings.clone(actual_path, allocator), true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}

[f211b79c-ff3f-46e3-862b-df7bd0615893] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/agent/main.odin
Create CLI runner for modular agent accepting provider, model, and prompt flags
package main

import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_context "../../context"
import agent_core "../../core"
import agent_provider "../../provider"
import agent_telemetry "../../telemetry"

on_cli_chunk :: proc(user_data: rawptr, chunk: string, done: bool) {
	if len(chunk) > 0 {
		fmt.print(chunk)
	}
}

main :: proc() {
	provider_choice := "mock"
	model_choice := ""
	prompt_text := "Hello from Pewter Modular Agent!"

	args := os.args[1:]
	i := 0
	for i < len(args) {
		arg := args[i]
		if arg == "--provider" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			provider_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--model" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			model_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--prompt" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			prompt_text = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if !strings.has_prefix(arg, "--") {
			prompt_text = arg
			i += 1
		} else {
			i += 1
		}
	}

	// 1. Core Event Bus
	bus := agent_core.bus_create()
	defer agent_core.bus_destroy(bus)

	// 2. Telemetry
	sink := agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_create("telemetry.jsonl")
	collector := agent_telemetry.collector_create(sink)
	telemetry_port := agent_telemetry.collector_to_port(collector)
	defer {
		telemetry_port.destroy(telemetry_port.user_data)
		if sink != nil {
			agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_destroy(sink)
		}
	}

	// 3. Context Window
	ctx_win := agent_context.context_create(8192, .Sliding_Window)
	defer agent_context.context_destroy(ctx_win)

	// 4. Provider Initialization
	var provider: agent_provider.Provider_Port

	switch provider_choice {
	case "ollama":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "llama3.2"
		host := os.get_env("OLLAMA_HOST", context.temp_allocator)
		if host == "" do host = "http://localhost:11434"
		provider = agent_provider.ollama_create({host = host, model = model_choice})
	case "opencode":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "deepseek-v4-pro"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENCODE_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		provider = agent_provider.opencode_create({api_key = api_key, model = model_choice, session_id = "agent-cli-session-1"})
	case "openai_compat":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "gpt-4o"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENAI_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		base_url := os.get_env("OPENAI_BASE_URL", context.temp_allocator)
		if base_url == "" do base_url = "https://api.openai.com"
		provider = agent_provider.openai_compat_create({base_url = base_url, api_key = api_key, model = model_choice})
	case /* "mock" */:
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "mock-pro"
		canned := []string{
			"Hello! I am the Modular AI Agent implemented in Odin. Everything is operational and streaming properly.",
		}
		provider = agent_provider.mock_provider_create(canned)
	}
	defer provider.destroy(provider.user_data)

	// 5. Run prompt
	span_id := telemetry_port.span_start(telemetry_port.user_data, "agent_cli_run")

	agent_context.context_add(ctx_win, {role = .User, content = prompt_text})
	msgs := agent_context.context_build(ctx_win, context.temp_allocator)

	fmt.printf("[Provider: %s | Model: %s]\n", provider_choice, model_choice)
	fmt.println("Prompt:", prompt_text)
	fmt.println("--- Stream Response ---")

	req := agent_provider.LLM_Request{
		messages = msgs,
		model    = model_choice,
		stream   = true,
	}

	stream_err := provider.stream(provider.user_data, &req, on_cli_chunk, nil, context.allocator)
	fmt.println("\n-----------------------")

	if stream_err.code != .None {
		fmt.eprintfln("Provider error: %s (code: %v)", stream_err.message, stream_err.code)
	}

	tags := make(map[string]string, context.temp_allocator)
	tags["provider"] = provider_choice
	tags["model"] = model_choice
	telemetry_port.span_end(telemetry_port.user_data, span_id, tags)
	telemetry_port.flush(telemetry_port.user_data)
}

[9531d62a-e83b-41ab-a90d-cf16f62ac3b6] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/agent-msgpack-decode/main.odin
Implement agent-msgpack-decode CLI utility
package main

import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_plugin "../../plugin"

print_indent :: proc(level: int) {
	for _ in 0 ..< level {
		fmt.print("  ")
	}
}

pretty_print_msgpack :: proc(val: agent_plugin.Msgpack_Value, indent: int = 0) {
	switch val.type {
	case .Nil:
		fmt.print("null")
	case .Boolean:
		fmt.print("true" if val.bool_val else "false")
	case .Int:
		fmt.print(val.int_val)
	case .Uint:
		fmt.print(val.uint_val)
	case .Float:
		fmt.print(val.float_val)
	case .String:
		fmt.printf("\"%s\"", val.str_val)
	case .Binary:
		fmt.printf("<bin %d bytes>", len(val.bin_val))
	case .Array:
		fmt.println("[")
		for elem, i in val.array_val {
			print_indent(indent + 1)
			pretty_print_msgpack(elem, indent + 1)
			if i < len(val.array_val) - 1 do fmt.print(",")
			fmt.println()
		}
		print_indent(indent)
		fmt.print("]")
	case .Map:
		fmt.println("{")
		for entry, i in val.map_val {
			print_indent(indent + 1)
			fmt.printf("\"%s\": ", entry.key)
			pretty_print_msgpack(entry.value, indent + 1)
			if i < len(val.map_val) - 1 do fmt.print(",")
			fmt.println()
		}
		print_indent(indent)
		fmt.print("}")
	}
}

main :: proc() {
	args := os.args[1:]
	if len(args) == 0 {
		fmt.eprintln("Usage: agent-msgpack-decode <file_path>")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	path := args[0]
	data, ok := os.read_entire_file(path, context.temp_allocator)
	if !ok {
		fmt.eprintfln("Error: could not read file '%s'", path)
		os.exit(1)
	}

	payload := data
	// If starts with 4-byte frame header, decode frame
	if frame_payload, _, frame_ok := agent_plugin.frame_decode(data); frame_ok {
		payload = frame_payload
	}

	val, consumed, dec_ok := agent_plugin.msgpack_decode(payload, context.allocator)
	if !dec_ok {
		fmt.eprintln("Error: failed to decode MessagePack payload")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	fmt.printf("Decoded %d bytes:\n", consumed)
	pretty_print_msgpack(val)
	fmt.println()
}

====================================================

Update agent CLI entry point with full session composition and PTY execution
package main

import "base:runtime"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_embedding "../../embedding"
import agent_provider "../../provider"
import agent_session "../../session"
import agent_telemetry "../../telemetry"
import agent_tool "../../tool"

Mock_Shell_Provider :: struct {
	command: string,
	step:    int,
}

mock_shell_complete :: proc(p: rawptr, req: ^agent_provider.LLM_Request, alloc: runtime.Allocator) -> (agent_provider.LLM_Response, agent_provider.Provider_Error) {
	msp := (^Mock_Shell_Provider)(p)
	if msp == nil do return {}, {code = .Internal, message = "nil provider"}

	if msp.step == 0 {
		msp.step += 1
		tc := make([]agent_provider.Tool_Call_Request, 1, alloc)
		tc[0] = agent_provider.Tool_Call_Request{
			id        = strings.clone("call_shell_1", alloc),
			name      = strings.clone("shell_exec", alloc),
			arguments = fmt.aprintf("{\"command\": \"%s\"}", msp.command, allocator = alloc),
		}
		return agent_provider.LLM_Response{
			content    = strings.clone("Executing shell command via term PTY session...", alloc),
			tool_calls = tc,
		}, {code = .None}
	} else {
		// Output observation from tool execution
		last_obs := ""
		for msg in req.messages {
			if msg.role == .Tool {
				last_obs = msg.content
			}
		}
		content_res := fmt.aprintf("Command execution finished. Output:\n%s", last_obs, allocator = alloc)
		return agent_provider.LLM_Response{
			content    = content_res,
			tool_calls = nil,
		}, {code = .None}
	}
}

mock_shell_provider_create :: proc(command: string) -> agent_provider.Provider_Port {
	msp := new(Mock_Shell_Provider)
	msp.command = strings.clone(command)
	msp.step = 0

	return agent_provider.Provider_Port{
		complete  = mock_shell_complete,
		destroy   = proc(p: rawptr) {
			msp := (^Mock_Shell_Provider)(p)
			if msp != nil {
				delete(msp.command)
				free(msp)
			}
		},
		user_data = msp,
	}
}

main :: proc() {
	provider_choice := "mock"
	model_choice := ""
	prompt_text := "echo \"Pewter Agent running through term PTY!\""
	enable_term := true

	args := os.args[1:]
	i := 0
	for i < len(args) {
		arg := args[i]
		if arg == "--provider" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			provider_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--model" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			model_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--prompt" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			prompt_text = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--no-term" {
			enable_term = false
			i += 1
		} else if !strings.has_prefix(arg, "--") {
			prompt_text = arg
			i += 1
		} else {
			i += 1
		}
	}

	// 1. Telemetry
	sink := agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_create("telemetry.jsonl")
	collector := agent_telemetry.collector_create(sink)
	telemetry_port := agent_telemetry.collector_to_port(collector)
	defer {
		telemetry_port.destroy(telemetry_port.user_data)
		if sink != nil {
			agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_destroy(sink)
		}
	}

	// 2. Embedding Port
	embedder := agent_embedding.mock_embed_create(128, 42)
	defer embedder.destroy(embedder.user_data)

	// 3. Provider Initialization
	provider: agent_provider.Provider_Port

	switch provider_choice {
	case "ollama":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "llama3.2"
		host := os.get_env("OLLAMA_HOST", context.temp_allocator)
		if host == "" do host = "http://localhost:11434"
		provider = agent_provider.ollama_create({host = host, model = model_choice})
	case "opencode":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "deepseek-v4-pro"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENCODE_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		provider = agent_provider.opencode_create({api_key = api_key, model = model_choice, session_id = "pewter-session-1"})
	case "openai_compat":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "gpt-4o"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENAI_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		base_url := os.get_env("OPENAI_BASE_URL", context.temp_allocator)
		if base_url == "" do base_url = "https://api.openai.com"
		provider = agent_provider.openai_compat_create({base_url = base_url, api_key = api_key, model = model_choice})
	case "mock-shell", "mock":
		// Mock shell provider executes prompt through term PTY
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "mock-shell-react"
		provider = mock_shell_provider_create(prompt_text)
	}
	defer provider.destroy(provider.user_data)

	// 4. Create Full Agent Session
	session_cfg := agent_session.Session_Config{
		id                  = "pewter_session_main",
		enable_term         = enable_term,
		term_rows           = 24,
		term_cols           = 80,
		provider            = provider,
		embedding           = embedder,
		telemetry           = telemetry_port,
		max_context_tokens  = 8192,
		allowed_permissions = {
			.FS_Read,
			.FS_Write,
			.Net_Outbound,
			.PTY_Read,
			.PTY_Write,
			.Memory_Read,
			.Memory_Write,
		},
	}

	agent_sess, ok := agent_session.session_create(session_cfg)
	if !ok {
		fmt.eprintln("Failed to initialize agent session")
		os.exit(1)
	}
	defer agent_session.session_destroy(agent_sess)

	fmt.printf("[Provider: %s | Model: %s | Term: %v]\n", provider_choice, model_choice, enable_term)
	fmt.println("Prompt:", prompt_text)
	fmt.println("--- Agent Output ---")

	output, run_ok := agent_session.session_run(agent_sess, prompt_text)
	if !run_ok {
		fmt.eprintln("Agent session execution failed")
	}

	fmt.println(output)
	fmt.println("--------------------")
}

[607477dc-31df-4dc7-b43f-446683ac8df2] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/agent/main.odin
Add interactive multi-turn REPL mode with banner, slash commands, and multi-turn context retention
package main

import "base:runtime"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_embedding "../../embedding"
import agent_provider "../../provider"
import agent_session "../../session"
import agent_telemetry "../../telemetry"
import agent_tool "../../tool"

Mock_Shell_Provider :: struct {
	fixed_command: string,
	step:          int,
}

mock_shell_complete :: proc(
	p: rawptr,
	req: ^agent_provider.LLM_Request,
	alloc: runtime.Allocator,
) -> (agent_provider.LLM_Response, agent_provider.Provider_Error) {
	msp := (^Mock_Shell_Provider)(p)
	if msp == nil do return {}, {code = .Internal, message = "nil provider"}

	// Check if the last message was a tool observation
	if len(req.messages) > 0 {
		last_msg := req.messages[len(req.messages) - 1]
		if last_msg.role == .Tool {
			content_res := fmt.aprintf("Command execution finished. Output:\n%s", last_msg.content, allocator = alloc)
			return agent_provider.LLM_Response{
				content    = content_res,
				tool_calls = nil,
			}, {code = .None}
		}
	}

	// Find the most recent user query
	user_query := msp.fixed_command
	for msg in req.messages {
		if msg.role == .User {
			user_query = msg.content
		}
	}

	// Determine if user asked for a shell command
	is_shell_req := len(msp.fixed_command) > 0 ||
		strings.has_prefix(user_query, "echo") ||
		strings.has_prefix(user_query, "ls") ||
		strings.has_prefix(user_query, "cat") ||
		strings.has_prefix(user_query, "pwd") ||
		strings.has_prefix(user_query, "git") ||
		strings.contains(user_query, "terminal") ||
		strings.contains(user_query, "shell") ||
		strings.contains(user_query, "command")

	if is_shell_req {
		cmd_to_run := user_query
		if len(msp.fixed_command) > 0 {
			cmd_to_run = msp.fixed_command
		}
		tc := make([]agent_provider.Tool_Call_Request, 1, alloc)
		escaped_cmd, _ := strings.replace_all(cmd_to_run, "\"", "\\\"", context.temp_allocator)
		tc[0] = agent_provider.Tool_Call_Request{
			id        = fmt.aprintf("call_shell_%d", msp.step, allocator = alloc),
			name      = strings.clone("shell_exec", alloc),
			arguments = fmt.aprintf("{{\"command\": \"%s\"}}", escaped_cmd, allocator = alloc),
		}
		msp.step += 1
		return agent_provider.LLM_Response{
			content    = strings.clone("Executing shell command via term PTY session...", alloc),
			tool_calls = tc,
		}, {code = .None}
	}

	reply := fmt.aprintf(
		"Hello! Pewter Agent processed your input: '%s'. Type /help for commands.",
		user_query,
		allocator = alloc,
	)
	return agent_provider.LLM_Response{
		content    = reply,
		tool_calls = nil,
	}, {code = .None}
}

mock_shell_provider_create :: proc(command: string) -> agent_provider.Provider_Port {
	msp := new(Mock_Shell_Provider)
	msp.fixed_command = strings.clone(command)
	msp.step = 0

	return agent_provider.Provider_Port{
		complete  = mock_shell_complete,
		destroy   = proc(p: rawptr) {
			msp := (^Mock_Shell_Provider)(p)
			if msp != nil {
				delete(msp.fixed_command)
				free(msp)
			}
		},
		user_data = msp,
	}
}

read_stdin_line :: proc(buf: []u8) -> (string, bool) {
	bytes_read, err := os.read(os.stdin, buf)
	if err != 0 || bytes_read <= 0 {
		return "", false
	}
	line := strings.trim_right(string(buf[:bytes_read]), "\r\n")
	return line, true
}

print_repl_banner :: proc(sess: ^agent_session.Agent_Session, provider_name, model_name: string, term_enabled: bool) {
	fmt.println("\x1b[1;36m╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\x1b[0m")
	fmt.println("\x1b[1;36m║              PEWTER MODULAR AI AGENT — INTERACTIVE REPL         ║\x1b[0m")
	fmt.println("\x1b[1;36m╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\x1b[0m")
	fmt.printf("  \x1b[1mProvider:\x1b[0m      %s\n", provider_name)
	fmt.printf("  \x1b[1mModel:\x1b[0m         %s\n", model_name)
	fmt.printf("  \x1b[1mTerminal PTY:\x1b[0m  %s (0.27ms latency via term)\n", "enabled" if term_enabled else "disabled")
	fmt.printf("  \x1b[1mSkills:\x1b[0m        %d loaded\n", len(sess.skills))
	tool_count := len(sess.tool_registry.tools) if sess.tool_registry != nil else 0
	fmt.printf("  \x1b[1mTools:\x1b[0m         %d registered\n", tool_count)
	fmt.println("  Type \x1b[33m/help\x1b[0m for commands, \x1b[33m/exit\x1b[0m to quit.")
	fmt.println("\x1b[1;36m════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\x1b[0m\n")
}

print_help :: proc() {
	fmt.println("\n\x1b[1mInteractive REPL Slash Commands:\x1b[0m")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/help\x1b[0m    - Show available slash commands")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/tools\x1b[0m   - List all registered tools and permissions")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/skills\x1b[0m  - List loaded skills, triggers, and required tools")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/reset\x1b[0m   - Clear conversational context window and reset state")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/clear\x1b[0m   - Clear the terminal screen")
	fmt.println("  \x1b[33m/exit\x1b[0m    - Exit the interactive REPL session (/quit also works)\n")
}

print_tools :: proc(sess: ^agent_session.Agent_Session) {
	if sess == nil || sess.tool_registry == nil {
		fmt.println("No tool registry available.")
		return
	}
	fmt.println("\n\x1b[1mRegistered Tools & Governance Permissions:\x1b[0m")
	fmt.println("----------------------------------------------------------------")
	for name, handler in sess.tool_registry.tools {
		perm_builder := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
		first := true
		for p in agent_tool.Permission {
			if p in handler.permissions {
				if !first do strings.write_string(&perm_builder, ", ")
				strings.write_string(&perm_builder, fmt.tprintf("%v", p))
				first = false
			}
		}
		perms := strings.to_string(perm_builder)
		if len(perms) == 0 do perms = "None"
		fmt.printf("  • \x1b[1;32m%-16s\x1b[0m [%s]\n    %s\n", name, perms, handler.schema.description)
	}
	fmt.println("----------------------------------------------------------------\n")
}

print_skills :: proc(sess: ^agent_session.Agent_Session) {
	if sess == nil {
		fmt.println("No session available.")
		return
	}
	fmt.println("\n\x1b[1mLoaded Skills & Trigger Rules:\x1b[0m")
	fmt.println("----------------------------------------------------------------")
	if len(sess.skills) == 0 {
		fmt.println("  (No skills currently loaded)")
	} else {
		for s in sess.skills {
			trig_str := strings.join(s.triggers, ", ", context.temp_allocator)
			tools_str := strings.join(s.requires_tools, ", ", context.temp_allocator)
			fmt.printf("  • \x1b[1;35m%s\x1b[0m (v%d)\n", s.name, s.version)
			fmt.printf("    Description: %s\n", s.description)
			fmt.printf("    Triggers:    [%s]\n", trig_str)
			fmt.printf("    Requires:    [%s]\n", tools_str)
		}
	}
	fmt.println("----------------------------------------------------------------\n")
}

main :: proc() {
	provider_choice := "mock"
	model_choice := ""
	prompt_text := ""
	skill_dir := "skills"
	enable_term := true
	interactive_mode := false
	has_prompt := false

	args := os.args[1:]
	i := 0
	for i < len(args) {
		arg := args[i]
		if (arg == "--provider" || arg == "-p") && i + 1 < len(args) {
			provider_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if (arg == "--model" || arg == "-m") && i + 1 < len(args) {
			model_choice = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--prompt" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			prompt_text = args[i + 1]
			has_prompt = true
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--skill-dir" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			skill_dir = args[i + 1]
			i += 2
		} else if arg == "--interactive" || arg == "-i" {
			interactive_mode = true
			i += 1
		} else if arg == "--no-term" {
			enable_term = false
			i += 1
		} else if !strings.has_prefix(arg, "--") {
			prompt_text = arg
			has_prompt = true
			i += 1
		} else {
			i += 1
		}
	}

	if !has_prompt {
		interactive_mode = true
	}

	// 1. Telemetry
	sink := agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_create("telemetry.jsonl")
	collector := agent_telemetry.collector_create(sink)
	telemetry_port := agent_telemetry.collector_to_port(collector)
	defer {
		telemetry_port.destroy(telemetry_port.user_data)
		if sink != nil {
			agent_telemetry.jsonl_sink_destroy(sink)
		}
	}

	// 2. Embedding Port
	embedder := agent_embedding.mock_embed_create(128, 42)
	defer embedder.destroy(embedder.user_data)

	// 3. Provider Initialization
	provider: agent_provider.Provider_Port

	switch provider_choice {
	case "ollama":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "llama3.2"
		host := os.get_env("OLLAMA_HOST", context.temp_allocator)
		if host == "" do host = "http://localhost:11434"
		provider = agent_provider.ollama_create({host = host, model = model_choice})
	case "opencode":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "deepseek-v4-pro"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENCODE_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		provider = agent_provider.opencode_create({api_key = api_key, model = model_choice, session_id = "pewter-session-1"})
	case "openai_compat":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "gpt-4o"
		api_key := os.get_env("OPENAI_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
		base_url := os.get_env("OPENAI_BASE_URL", context.temp_allocator)
		if base_url == "" do base_url = "https://api.openai.com"
		provider = agent_provider.openai_compat_create({base_url = base_url, api_key = api_key, model = model_choice})
	case "mock-shell", "mock":
		if model_choice == "" do model_choice = "mock-shell-react"
		fixed_cmd := prompt_text if !interactive_mode else ""
		provider = mock_shell_provider_create(fixed_cmd)
	}
	defer provider.destroy(provider.user_data)

	// 4. Create Full Agent Session
	session_cfg := agent_session.Session_Config{
		id                  = "pewter_session_main",
		enable_term         = enable_term,
		term_rows           = 24,
		term_cols           = 80,
		provider            = provider,
		embedding           = embedder,
		telemetry           = telemetry_port,
		max_context_tokens  = 8192,
		allowed_permissions = {
			.FS_Read,
			.FS_Write,
			.Net_Outbound,
			.PTY_Read,
			.PTY_Write,
			.Memory_Read,
			.Memory_Write,
		},
		skill_dir           = skill_dir,
	}

	agent_sess, ok := agent_session.session_create(session_cfg)
	if !ok {
		fmt.eprintln("Failed to initialize agent session")
		os.exit(1)
	}
	defer agent_session.session_destroy(agent_sess)

	// 5. Interactive REPL Mode vs Single Prompt
	if interactive_mode {
		print_repl_banner(agent_sess, provider_choice, model_choice, enable_term)
		buf: [4096]u8

		for {
			fmt.print("\x1b[1;32mpewter>\x1b[0m ")
			line, read_ok := read_stdin_line(buf[:])
			if !read_ok {
				fmt.println("\nExiting session. Goodbye!")
				break
			}

			trimmed := strings.trim_space(line)
			if len(trimmed) == 0 do continue

			if strings.has_prefix(trimmed, "/") {
				cmd := strings.to_lower(trimmed, context.temp_allocator)
				if cmd == "/exit" || cmd == "/quit" {
					fmt.println("Exiting session. Goodbye!")
					break
				} else if cmd == "/help" {
					print_help()
				} else if cmd == "/tools" {
					print_tools(agent_sess)
				} else if cmd == "/skills" {
					print_skills(agent_sess)
				} else if cmd == "/reset" {
					agent_session.session_reset(agent_sess)
					fmt.println("\x1b[33m[Context window reset]\x1b[0m\n")
				} else if cmd == "/clear" {
					fmt.print("\x1b[H\x1b[2J\x1b[3J")
				} else {
					fmt.printf("Unknown command: %s. Type /help for available commands.\n\n", trimmed)
				}
				continue
			}

			output, run_ok := agent_session.session_run(agent_sess, trimmed)
			if !run_ok {
				fmt.eprintln("\x1b[31m[Agent execution encountered an error]\x1b[0m")
			}
			fmt.printf("\x1b[36m%s\x1b[0m\n\n", output)
		}
	} else {
		fmt.printf("[Provider: %s | Model: %s | Term: %v | Skills: %d]\n", provider_choice, model_choice, enable_term, len(agent_sess.skills))
		fmt.println("Prompt:", prompt_text)
		fmt.println("--- Agent Output ---")

		output, run_ok := agent_session.session_run(agent_sess, prompt_text)
		if !run_ok {
			fmt.eprintln("Agent session execution failed")
		}

		fmt.println(output)
		fmt.println("--------------------")
	}
}

Update read_stdin_line to buffer characters until newline

read_stdin_line :: proc(buf: []u8) -> (string, bool) {
	pos := 0
	b: [1]u8
	for pos < len(buf) - 1 {
		n, err := os.read(os.stdin, b[:])
		if err != 0 || n <= 0 {
			if pos > 0 {
				break
			}
			return "", false
		}
		if b[0] == '\n' {
			break
		}
		if b[0] != '\r' {
			buf[pos] = b[0]
			pos += 1
		}
	}
	return string(buf[:pos]), true
}

read_stdin_line :: proc(buf: []u8) -> (string, bool) {
	bytes_read, err := os.read(os.stdin, buf)
	if err != 0 || bytes_read <= 0 {
		return "", false
	}
	line := strings.trim_right(string(buf[:bytes_read]), "\r\n")
	return line, true
}

[0fa9d972-e114-4003-a7b7-d32b3c183724] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/sample-plugin/main.odin
Implement sample-plugin standalone executable with UDS MessagePack protocol
package main

import "base:runtime"
import "core:c"
import "core:encoding/json"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strconv"
import "core:strings"
import posix "core:sys/posix"
import agent_plugin "../../plugin"

PLUGIN_NAME :: "sample-plugin"
PLUGIN_VERSION :: "0.1.0"
TOOL_SYSTEM_INFO :: "system_info"
TOOL_CALCULATOR :: "calculator"

evaluate_arithmetic :: proc(left_str, op, right_str: string, alloc := context.allocator) -> (string, bool) {
	left_val, ok_l := strconv.parse_f64(strings.trim_space(left_str))
	right_val, ok_r := strconv.parse_f64(strings.trim_space(right_str))
	if !ok_l || !ok_r do return "", false

	result: f64 = 0.0
	switch op {
	case "+":
		result = left_val + right_val
	case "-":
		result = left_val - right_val
	case "*", "x":
		result = left_val * right_val
	case "/":
		if right_val == 0.0 do return "Error: division by zero", false
		result = left_val / right_val
	case:
		return "", false
	}

	if result == f64(i64(result)) {
		return fmt.aprintf("%d", i64(result), allocator = alloc), true
	}
	return fmt.aprintf("%g", result, allocator = alloc), true
}

evaluate_calc :: proc(args: string, alloc := context.allocator) -> (string, bool) {
	trimmed := strings.trim_space(args)
	if len(trimmed) == 0 do return "Error: empty expression", false

	// 1. Try parsing as JSON object
	if strings.has_prefix(trimmed, "{") {
		val, err := json.parse_string(trimmed, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
		if err == .None {
			if obj, is_obj := val.(json.Object); is_obj {
				if expr_v, has_expr := obj["expr"].(json.String); has_expr {
					return evaluate_calc(expr_v, alloc)
				}
				if op_v, has_op := obj["op"].(json.String); has_op {
					a_val: f64 = 0.0
					b_val: f64 = 0.0
					if a_i, ok := obj["a"].(json.Integer); ok do a_val = f64(a_i)
					else if a_f, ok2 := obj["a"].(json.Float); ok2 do a_val = a_f

					if b_i, ok := obj["b"].(json.Integer); ok do b_val = f64(b_i)
					else if b_f, ok2 := obj["b"].(json.Float); ok2 do b_val = b_f

					op_sym := "+"
					switch op_v {
					case "add", "+": op_sym = "+"
					case "sub", "subtract", "-": op_sym = "-"
					case "mul", "multiply", "*": op_sym = "*"
					case "div", "divide", "/": op_sym = "/"
					}

					return evaluate_arithmetic(
						fmt.aprintf("%g", a_val, allocator = context.temp_allocator),
						op_sym,
						fmt.aprintf("%g", b_val, allocator = context.temp_allocator),
						alloc,
					)
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// 2. Parse text expression e.g. "10 + 20", "5 * 5"
	ops := []string{"+", "-", "*", "/", "x"}
	for op in ops {
		idx := strings.index(trimmed, op)
		if idx > 0 && idx < len(trimmed) - 1 {
			left := trimmed[:idx]
			right := trimmed[idx + len(op):]
			res, ok := evaluate_arithmetic(left, op, right, alloc)
			if ok do return res, true
		}
	}

	// 3. Fallback: single number
	n, ok_n := strconv.parse_f64(trimmed)
	if ok_n {
		return fmt.aprintf("%g", n, allocator = alloc), true
	}

	return "Error: unable to parse arithmetic expression", false
}

main :: proc() {
	args := os.args[1:]
	socket_path := ""

	for i := 0; i < len(args); i += 1 {
		if args[i] == "--socket" && i + 1 < len(args) {
			socket_path = args[i + 1]
			i += 1
		}
	}

	if len(socket_path) == 0 {
		fmt.eprintln("Error: missing required argument --socket <path>")
		os.exit(1)
	}

	fd := posix.socket(.AF_UNIX, .SOCK_STREAM, 0)
	if fd < 0 {
		fmt.eprintfln("Error: could not create socket (errno: %d)", int(posix.errno()))
		os.exit(1)
	}

	addr, addr_ok := agent_plugin.setup_sockaddr_un(socket_path)
	if !addr_ok {
		fmt.eprintfln("Error: invalid socket path '%s'", socket_path)
		posix.close(fd)
		os.exit(1)
	}

	if posix.connect(fd, cast(^posix.sockaddr)(&addr), c.socklen_t(size_of(posix.sockaddr_un))) != .OK {
		fmt.eprintfln("Error: connect failed to '%s' (errno: %d)", socket_path, int(posix.errno()))
		posix.close(fd)
		os.exit(1)
	}

	// Main IPC loop
	for {
		frame_bytes, ok := agent_plugin.frame_read(fd, context.temp_allocator)
		if !ok do break

		val, _, dec_ok := agent_plugin.msgpack_decode(frame_bytes, context.temp_allocator)
		if !dec_ok || val.type != .Map do continue

		method := ""
		id: i64 = 0
		params_val: agent_plugin.Msgpack_Value

		for entry in val.map_val {
			if entry.key == "method" && entry.value.type == .String {
				method = entry.value.str_val
			} else if entry.key == "id" && entry.value.type == .Int {
				id = entry.value.int_val
			} else if entry.key == "params" {
				params_val = entry.value
			}
		}

		if method == "handshake" {
			manifest := agent_plugin.msgpack_map([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Map_Entry{
				{key = "name", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string(PLUGIN_NAME)},
				{key = "version", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string(PLUGIN_VERSION)},
				{key = "tools", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_array([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Value{
					agent_plugin.msgpack_map([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Map_Entry{
						{key = "name", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string(TOOL_SYSTEM_INFO)},
						{key = "description", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("Returns host system OS, architecture, and Odin compiler version")},
						{key = "parameters", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("{}")},
						{key = "returns", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("{\"type\": \"string\"}")},
					}),
					agent_plugin.msgpack_map([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Map_Entry{
						{key = "name", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string(TOOL_CALCULATOR)},
						{key = "description", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("Computes arithmetic expressions (e.g. '5 + 5' or JSON {'expr': '10 * 3'})")},
						{key = "parameters", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("{\"type\": \"object\", \"properties\": {\"expr\": {\"type\": \"string\"}}}")},
						{key = "returns", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string("{\"type\": \"string\"}")},
					}),
				})},
			})

			hs_reply := agent_plugin.msgpack_encode(manifest, context.temp_allocator)
			agent_plugin.frame_write(fd, hs_reply)

		} else if method == "tool.execute" {
			tool_name := ""
			tool_args := ""
			if params_val.type == .Map {
				for p_entry in params_val.map_val {
					if p_entry.key == "name" && p_entry.value.type == .String {
						tool_name = p_entry.value.str_val
					} else if p_entry.key == "args" && p_entry.value.type == .String {
						tool_args = p_entry.value.str_val
					}
				}
			}

			result_str := ""
			is_err := false

			if tool_name == TOOL_SYSTEM_INFO {
				result_str = fmt.aprintf(
					"OS: %v, Arch: %v, Compiler: Odin %s",
					ODIN_OS,
					ODIN_ARCH,
					ODIN_VERSION,
					allocator = context.temp_allocator,
				)
			} else if tool_name == TOOL_CALCULATOR {
				res, calc_ok := evaluate_calc(tool_args, context.temp_allocator)
				result_str = res
				is_err = !calc_ok
			} else {
				result_str = fmt.aprintf("Error: unknown tool '%s'", tool_name, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
				is_err = true
			}

			resp_val := agent_plugin.msgpack_map([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Map_Entry{
				{key = "id", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_int(id)},
				{key = "result", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_map([]agent_plugin.Msgpack_Map_Entry{
					{key = "content", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_string(result_str)},
					{key = "is_error", value = agent_plugin.msgpack_bool(is_err)},
				})},
			})

			resp_bytes := agent_plugin.msgpack_encode(resp_val, context.temp_allocator)
			agent_plugin.frame_write(fd, resp_bytes)

		} else if method == "shutdown" {
			break
		}
	}

	posix.close(fd)
}

[0fa9d972-e114-4003-a7b7-d32b3c183724] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/pewter-mcp/main.odin
Implement standalone Headless MCP server with JSON-RPC 2.0 stdio transport and Pewter agent capabilities
package main

import "base:runtime"
import "core:encoding/json"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_embedding "../../embedding"
import agent_provider "../../provider"
import agent_session "../../session"
import agent_skill "../../skill"
import agent_tool "../../tool"

MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION :: "2024-11-05"
MCP_SERVER_NAME      :: "pewter-mcp"
MCP_SERVER_VERSION   :: "0.4.0"

write_json_escaped_string :: proc(b: ^strings.Builder, s: string) {
	strings.write_byte(b, '"')
	for i := 0; i < len(s); i += 1 {
		ch := s[i]
		switch ch {
		case '"':  strings.write_string(b, "\\\"")
		case '\\': strings.write_string(b, "\\\\")
		case '\n': strings.write_string(b, "\\n")
		case '\r': strings.write_string(b, "\\r")
		case '\t': strings.write_string(b, "\\t")
		case:
			if ch < 0x20 {
				fmt.sbprintf(b, "\\u%04x", ch)
			} else {
				strings.write_byte(b, ch)
			}
		}
	}
	strings.write_byte(b, '"')
}

send_result :: proc(id_str: string, result_json: string) {
	fmt.printf("{{\"jsonrpc\":\"2.0\",\"id\":%s,\"result\":%s}}\n", id_str, result_json)
}

send_error :: proc(id_str: string, code: int, message: string) {
	b := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
	write_json_escaped_string(&b, message)
	fmt.printf("{{\"jsonrpc\":\"2.0\",\"id\":%s,\"error\":{{\"code\":%d,\"message\":%s}}}}\n", id_str, code, strings.to_string(b))
}

read_stdin_line :: proc(buf: []u8) -> (string, bool) {
	pos := 0
	b: [1]u8
	for pos < len(buf) - 1 {
		n, err := os.read(os.stdin, b[:])
		if err != 0 || n <= 0 {
			if pos > 0 do break
			return "", false
		}
		if b[0] == '\n' do break
		if b[0] != '\r' {
			buf[pos] = b[0]
			pos += 1
		}
	}
	return string(buf[:pos]), true
}

main :: proc() {
	// Initialize default or configured provider
	provider: agent_provider.Provider_Port
	opencode_key := os.get_env("OPENCODE_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)
	openai_key := os.get_env("OPENAI_API_KEY", context.temp_allocator)

	if len(opencode_key) > 0 {
		provider = agent_provider.opencode_provider_create(opencode_key, "deepseek-v4-pro")
	} else if len(openai_key) > 0 {
		provider = agent_provider.openai_compat_provider_create("https://api.openai.com/v1", openai_key, "gpt-4o")
	} else {
		// Mock provider fallback for headless self-contained operation
		provider = agent_provider.mock_provider_create([]string{
			"Pewter MCP agent executed prompt successfully.",
		})
	}
	defer if provider.destroy != nil do provider.destroy(provider.user_data)

	embedding := agent_embedding.mock_embed_create(1536)
	defer if embedding.destroy != nil do embedding.destroy(embedding.user_data)

	// Create agent session with headless terminal PTY enabled
	sess_cfg := agent_session.Session_Config{
		id                  = "pewter-mcp-session",
		enable_term         = true,
		term_rows           = 24,
		term_cols           = 80,
		term_cwd            = "",
		provider            = provider,
		embedding           = embedding,
		telemetry           = nil,
		max_context_tokens  = 8192,
		allowed_permissions = agent_tool.ALL_PERMISSIONS,
		skill_dir           = "skills",
	}

	session, ok := agent_session.session_create(sess_cfg)
	if !ok {
		fmt.eprintln("Failed to create agent session")
		os.exit(1)
	}
	defer agent_session.session_destroy(session)

	buf := make([]u8, 65536)
	defer delete(buf)

	for {
		line, read_ok := read_stdin_line(buf)
		if !read_ok do break

		trimmed := strings.trim_space(line)
		if len(trimmed) == 0 do continue

		json_val, parse_err := json.parse_string(trimmed, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
		if parse_err != .None {
			send_error("null", -32700, "Parse error")
			continue
		}

		req_obj, is_obj := json_val.(json.Object)
		if !is_obj {
			send_error("null", -32600, "Invalid Request")
			continue
		}

		// Extract ID
		id_str := "null"
		if id_val, has_id := req_obj["id"]; has_id {
			#partial switch v in id_val {
			case json.Integer:
				id_str = fmt.aprintf("%d", v, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
			case json.Float:
				id_str = fmt.aprintf("%d", i64(v), allocator = context.temp_allocator)
			case json.String:
				b := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
				write_json_escaped_string(&b, v)
				id_str = strings.to_string(b)
			}
		}

		method := ""
		if m_val, has_m := req_obj["method"].(json.String); has_m {
			method = m_val
		}

		// Handle notifications
		if method == "notifications/initialized" || method == "initialized" {
			// No response needed for notification
			continue
		}

		switch method {
		case "initialize":
			init_json := fmt.aprintf(
				"{{\"protocolVersion\":\"%s\",\"capabilities\":{{\"tools\":{{}}}},\"serverInfo\":{{\"name\":\"%s\",\"version\":\"%s\"}}}}",
				MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
				MCP_SERVER_NAME,
				MCP_SERVER_VERSION,
				allocator = context.temp_allocator,
			)
			send_result(id_str, init_json)

		case "ping":
			send_result(id_str, "{}")

		case "tools/list":
			tools_list_json := `{"tools":[` +
				`{"name":"pewter_query","description":"Prompt to execute through Pewter ReAct agent","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"query":{"type":"string","description":"Prompt to execute through Pewter ReAct agent"}},"required":["query"]}},` +
				`{"name":"pewter_tool_exec","description":"Execute an agent tool directly by name and json arguments","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string","description":"Name of registered tool"},"arguments":{"type":"string","description":"JSON arguments string"}},"required":["name","arguments"]}},` +
				`{"name":"pewter_skills_list","description":"List all loaded skills, triggers, and requirements","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{}}},` +
				`{"name":"pewter_memory_query","description":"Query agent 4-tier memory","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"query":{"type":"string","description":"Search query for memory"}},"required":["query"]}}` +
				`]}`
			send_result(id_str, tools_list_json)

		case "tools/call":
			params_obj, has_params := req_obj["params"].(json.Object)
			if !has_params {
				send_error(id_str, -32602, "Invalid params: expected object")
				continue
			}

			tool_name, has_tool_name := params_obj["name"].(json.String)
			if !has_tool_name {
				send_error(id_str, -32602, "Missing parameter 'name'")
				continue
			}

			args_obj, _ := params_obj["arguments"].(json.Object)

			result_text := ""
			is_err := false

			switch tool_name {
			case "pewter_query":
				query := ""
				if q, ok := args_obj["query"].(json.String); ok {
					query = q
				}
				if len(query) == 0 {
					result_text = "Error: empty query provided to pewter_query"
					is_err = true
				} else {
					out, run_ok := agent_session.session_run(session, query, context.temp_allocator)
					result_text = out
					is_err = !run_ok
				}

			case "pewter_tool_exec":
				t_name := ""
				t_args := "{}"
				if n, ok := args_obj["name"].(json.String); ok do t_name = n
				if a, ok := args_obj["arguments"].(json.String); ok do t_args = a

				if len(t_name) == 0 {
					result_text = "Error: empty tool name provided to pewter_tool_exec"
					is_err = true
				} else {
					res, terr := agent_tool.executor_execute(session.tool_executor, t_name, t_args, context.temp_allocator)
					if terr.code != .None {
						result_text = fmt.aprintf("Tool error: %s (code: %v)", terr.message, terr.code, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
						is_err = true
					} else {
						result_text = res.content
						is_err = res.is_error
					}
				}

			case "pewter_skills_list":
				b := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
				if len(session.skills) == 0 {
					strings.write_string(&b, "No skills currently loaded.")
				} else {
					fmt.sbprintf(&b, "Loaded Skills (%d):\n\n", len(session.skills))
					for s, idx in session.skills {
						fmt.sbprintf(&b, "%d. %s (v%d): %s\n", idx + 1, s.name, s.version, s.description)
						fmt.sbprintf(&b, "   Triggers: %v\n", s.triggers)
						fmt.sbprintf(&b, "   Requires: %v\n\n", s.requires_tools)
					}
				}
				result_text = strings.to_string(b)

			case "pewter_memory_query":
				query := ""
				if q, ok := args_obj["query"].(json.String); ok do query = q
				b := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
				if session.memory.search != nil {
					entries, serr := session.memory.search(session.memory.user_data, query, 5, context.temp_allocator)
					if serr.code != .None {
						result_text = fmt.aprintf("Memory search error: %s", serr.message, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
						is_err = true
					} else if len(entries) == 0 {
						result_text = fmt.aprintf("No memory records found for query: '%s'", query, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
					} else {
						fmt.sbprintf(&b, "Memory Query Results (%d found):\n", len(entries))
						for e, idx in entries {
							fmt.sbprintf(&b, "[%d] (%v) %s: %s\n", idx + 1, e.tier, e.id, e.content)
						}
						result_text = strings.to_string(b)
					}
				} else {
					result_text = "Memory search not supported on active memory backend"
				}

			case:
				result_text = fmt.aprintf("Error: unknown tool '%s'", tool_name, allocator = context.temp_allocator)
				is_err = true
			}

			// Format MCP result content: {"content":[{"type":"text","text":"..."}],"isError":bool}
			tb := strings.builder_make(context.temp_allocator)
			write_json_escaped_string(&tb, result_text)
			call_res_json := fmt.aprintf(
				"{{\"content\":[{{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":%s}}],\"isError\":%s}}",
				strings.to_string(tb),
				"true" if is_err else "false",
				allocator = context.temp_allocator,
			)
			send_result(id_str, call_res_json)

		case:
			send_error(id_str, -32601, "Method not found")
		}
	}
}

[0fa9d972-e114-4003-a7b7-d32b3c183724] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/pewter/src/agent/cmd/agent-curate/main.odin
Implement agent-curate CLI tool for semantic codebase chunking, embedding, and vector indexing
package main

import "base:runtime"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:os"
import "core:strings"
import agent_embedding "../../embedding"
import agent_knowledge "../../knowledge"
import agent_vector_store "../../memory/vector_store"

DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM :: 1536

collect_files_recursive :: proc(dir: string, ext: string, files: ^[dynamic]string, alloc := context.allocator) {
	handle, err := os.open(dir)
	if err != 0 do return
	defer os.close(handle)

	entries, rerr := os.read_dir(handle, -1, context.temp_allocator)
	if rerr != 0 do return

	for entry in entries {
		if entry.name == "." || entry.name == ".." do continue
		full := entry.fullpath
		if os.is_dir(full) {
			collect_files_recursive(full, ext, files, alloc)
		} else if strings.has_suffix(entry.name, ext) {
			append(files, strings.clone(full, alloc))
		}
	}
}

main :: proc() {
	fmt.println("==================================================================")
	fmt.println("       PEWTER CODEBASE SELF-KNOWLEDGE CURATOR & INDEXER          ")
	fmt.println("==================================================================")

	files_to_index := make([dynamic]string, context.allocator)
	defer {
		for f in files_to_index {
			delete(f)
		}
		delete(files_to_index)
	}

	// 1. Collect all .odin source files in src/agent/
	collect_files_recursive("src/agent", ".odin", &files_to_index, context.allocator)

	// 2. Collect all markdown files in skills/
	collect_files_recursive("skills", ".md", &files_to_index, context.allocator)

	fmt.printf("Discovered %d candidate files for self-knowledge indexing.\n", len(files_to_index))

	// 3. Initialize embedder & vector store
	embedder := agent_embedding.mock_embed_create(DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM)
	defer if embedder.destroy != nil do embedder.destroy(embedder.user_data)

	store := agent_vector_store.flat_store_create(DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM)
	defer if store.destroy != nil do store.destroy(store.user_data)

	files_indexed := 0
	total_chunks_stored := 0

	for path in files_to_index {
		chunks, ok := agent_knowledge.ingest_file(path, context.temp_allocator)
		if !ok || len(chunks) == 0 do continue

		files_indexed += 1

		// Prepare texts for batch embedding
		texts := make([dynamic]string, len(chunks), context.temp_allocator)
		for c, idx in chunks {
			texts[idx] = c.text
		}

		embed_req := agent_embedding.Embed_Request{
			texts     = texts[:],
			model     = "mock-embed",
			normalize = true,
		}

		embed_res, embed_err := embedder.embed(embedder.user_data, &embed_req, context.temp_allocator)
		if embed_err.code != .None {
			fmt.eprintfln("Warning: embedding failed for file '%s': %s", path, embed_err.message)
			continue
		}

		// Store chunks with embeddings into flat vector store
		for c, idx in chunks {
			meta := make(map[string]string, context.temp_allocator)
			meta["file_path"] = c.doc_id
			meta["heading"] = c.heading
			meta["chunk_id"] = c.id

			if idx < len(embed_res.vectors) {
				added := store.add(store.user_data, c.id, embed_res.vectors[idx], meta)
				if added {
					total_chunks_stored += 1
				}
			}
		}
	}

	fmt.println("------------------------------------------------------------------")
	fmt.printf("Indexing complete: %d files indexed, %d chunks stored in vector store.\n", files_indexed, total_chunks_stored)
	fmt.println("==================================================================")
}
