[cfc5d918-31c6-4024-a008-3e34b08aa277] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/bench/cmd_parser/main.odin
Allocate Terminal on heap, update calls passing &t to t, and format duration cleanly in ms with cast(int)
--- REPLACEMENT:
	p: parser.Parser
	parser.parser_init(&p)
	
	t := new(termgrid.Terminal)
	termgrid.terminal_init(t, 24, 80)
	defer {
		termgrid.terminal_destroy(t)
		free(t)
	}
	
	// Test 1: ASCII throughput
	fmt.println("=== Performance Test 1: ASCII Throughput ===")
	
	// Create a large ASCII buffer (1 MB)
	ascii_data := make([]u8, 1024 * 1024)
	for i in 0..<len(ascii_data) {
		ascii_data[i] = u8(0x20 + (i % 95)) // printable ASCII
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, ascii_data[:1024])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start := time.now()
	iterations := 100
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, ascii_data)
	}
	elapsed := time.since(start)
	
	total_bytes := int(len(ascii_data)) * iterations
	mb_per_sec := f64(total_bytes) / f64(elapsed / time.Millisecond) / 1024.0
	cycles_per_byte := f64(elapsed) / f64(total_bytes)
	
	fmt.printf("Processed %d bytes in %d ms\n", total_bytes, cast(int)(elapsed / time.Millisecond))
	fmt.printf("Throughput: %.2f MB/s\n", mb_per_sec)
	fmt.printf("Cycles/byte: %.2f ns/byte\n", cycles_per_byte)
	fmt.printf("Target: >100 MB/s, <10 ns/byte\n")
	
	if mb_per_sec > 100.0 {
		fmt.println("✓ ASCII throughput target met")
	} else {
		fmt.println("✗ ASCII throughput target NOT met")
	}
	
	delete(ascii_data)
	
	// Test 2: CSI sequence throughput
	fmt.println("\n=== Performance Test 2: CSI Sequence Throughput ===")
	
	// Create CSI sequence buffer (ESC[1;1H repeated)
	csi_data := make([]u8, 10000)
	pos := 0
	for i in 0..<1000 {
		csi_data[pos+0] = 0x1B
		csi_data[pos+1] = '['
		csi_data[pos+2] = '1'
		csi_data[pos+3] = ';'
		csi_data[pos+4] = '1'
		csi_data[pos+5] = 'H'
		pos += 6
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, csi_data[:60])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start = time.now()
	iterations = 1000
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, csi_data)
	}
	elapsed = time.since(start)
	
	sequences_per_sec := f64(1000 * iterations) / (f64(elapsed) / f64(time.Second))
	
	fmt.printf("Processed %d sequences in %d ms\n", 1000 * iterations, cast(int)(elapsed / time.Millisecond))
	fmt.printf("Throughput: %.0f sequences/sec\n", sequences_per_sec)
	fmt.printf("Target: >1M sequences/sec\n")
	
	if sequences_per_sec > 1_000_000.0 {
		fmt.println("✓ CSI sequence throughput target met")
	} else {
		fmt.println("✗ CSI sequence throughput target NOT met")
	}
	
	delete(csi_data)
	
	// Test 3: Mixed workload (95% ASCII, 5% CSI)
	fmt.println("\n=== Performance Test 3: Mixed Workload ===")
	
	mixed_data := make([]u8, 100000)
	pos = 0
	for i in 0..<1000 {
		// 95 bytes of ASCII
		for j in 0..<95 {
			mixed_data[pos] = u8(0x20 + (j % 95))
			pos += 1
		}
		// 5 bytes of CSI (ESC[0m)
		mixed_data[pos+0] = 0x1B
		mixed_data[pos+1] = '['
		mixed_data[pos+2] = '0'
		mixed_data[pos+3] = 'm'
		pos += 4
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, mixed_data[:1000])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start = time.now()
	iterations = 100
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, t, mixed_data)
	}
	elapsed = time.since(start)

	// Test 1: ASCII throughput
	fmt.println("=== Performance Test 1: ASCII Throughput ===")
	
	// Create a large ASCII buffer (1 MB)
	ascii_data := make([]u8, 1024 * 1024)
	for i in 0..<len(ascii_data) {
		ascii_data[i] = u8(0x20 + (i % 95)) // printable ASCII
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, ascii_data[:1024])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start := time.now()
	iterations := 100
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, ascii_data)
	}
	elapsed := time.since(start)
	
	total_bytes := int(len(ascii_data)) * iterations
	mb_per_sec := f64(total_bytes) / f64(elapsed / time.Millisecond) / 1024.0
	cycles_per_byte := f64(elapsed) / f64(total_bytes)
	
	fmt.printf("Processed %d bytes in %d ms\n", total_bytes, elapsed / time.Millisecond)
	fmt.printf("Throughput: %.2f MB/s\n", mb_per_sec)
	fmt.printf("Cycles/byte: %.2f ns/byte\n", cycles_per_byte)
	fmt.printf("Target: >100 MB/s, <10 ns/byte\n")
	
	if mb_per_sec > 100.0 {
		fmt.println("✓ ASCII throughput target met")
	} else {
		fmt.println("✗ ASCII throughput target NOT met")
	}

	// Test 2: CSI sequence throughput
	fmt.println("\n=== Performance Test 2: CSI Sequence Throughput ===")
	
	// Create CSI sequence buffer (ESC[1;1H repeated)
	csi_data := make([]u8, 10000)
	pos := 0
	for i in 0..<1000 {
		csi_data[pos+0] = 0x1B
		csi_data[pos+1] = '['
		csi_data[pos+2] = '1'
		csi_data[pos+3] = ';'
		csi_data[pos+4] = '1'
		csi_data[pos+5] = 'H'
		pos += 6
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, csi_data[:60])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start = time.now()
	iterations = 1000
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, csi_data)
	}
	elapsed = time.since(start)
	
	sequences_per_sec := f64(1000 * iterations) / (f64(elapsed) / f64(time.Second))
	
	fmt.printf("Processed %d sequences in %d ms\n", 1000 * iterations, elapsed / time.Millisecond)
	fmt.printf("Throughput: %.0f sequences/sec\n", sequences_per_sec)
	fmt.printf("Target: >1M sequences/sec\n")

		fmt.println("✓ CSI sequence throughput target met")
	} else {
		fmt.println("✗ CSI sequence throughput target NOT met")
	}

	// Test 3: Mixed workload (95% ASCII, 5% CSI)
	fmt.println("\n=== Performance Test 3: Mixed Workload ===")
	
	mixed_data := make([]u8, 100000)
	pos = 0
	for i in 0..<1000 {
		// 95 bytes of ASCII
		for j in 0..<95 {
			mixed_data[pos] = u8(0x20 + (j % 95))
			pos += 1
		}
		// 5 bytes of CSI (ESC[0m)
		mixed_data[pos+0] = 0x1B
		mixed_data[pos+1] = '['
		mixed_data[pos+2] = '0'
		mixed_data[pos+3] = 'm'
		pos += 4
	}
	
	// Warm up
	parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, mixed_data[:1000])
	parser.parser_reset(&p)
	
	// Benchmark
	start = time.now()
	iterations = 100
	for i in 0..<iterations {
		parser.parser_reset(&p)
		parser.parse_chunk(&p, &t, mixed_data)
	}
	elapsed = time.since(start)

	mb_per_sec = f64(total_bytes) / f64(elapsed / time.Millisecond) / 1024.0

	fmt.printf("Throughput: %.2f MB/s\n", mb_per_sec)
	fmt.printf("Target: >100 MB/s for typical terminal output\n")
	
	if mb_per_sec > 100.0 {
		fmt.println("✓ Mixed workload throughput target met")
	} else {
		fmt.println("✗ Mixed workload throughput target NOT met")
	}
	
	delete(mixed_data)
	
	fmt.println("\n=== All Performance Tests Complete ===")

[5fd293d4-1d54-4f4c-a6eb-bbaacb2678b7] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Change poll timeout to 10 and add _app_on_backend_data_ready procedure
--- REPLACEMENT:
		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 10)
		if r > 0 {
			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				}
				time.sleep(1 * time.Millisecond)
			}
		}
	}
}

_app_on_backend_data_ready :: proc(user_data: rawptr) {
	a := (^App)(user_data)
	if a == nil do return
	if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
		user_ev: sdl3.Event
		user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
		_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
	}
}
--- TARGET:
		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 50)
		if r > 0 {
			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				}
				time.sleep(1 * time.Millisecond)
			}
		}
	}
}

	has_keyboard_input := false

		evs: [input.INPUT_PUMP_MAX_EVENTS]input.Input_Event
		first_ev: ^sdl3.Event = nil
		if a.has_wake_event {
			first_ev = &a.pending_wake_event
			a.has_wake_event = false
		}

				has_keyboard_input = true
				break
			}
		}
		quit, _ := app_dispatch_input_events(a, evs[:n_events])
		if quit {
			a.should_quit = true
			return false
		}

			win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)
			a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		}

		if active_b == nil do return false
		_app_sync_focus(a)
		resized := app_on_resize(a, a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h)
		_ = _app_apply_zoom(a)
		if resized && len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 {
			if session_poll_all(&a.session_mgr, 65536) {
				any_damaged = true
			}
		}
	}

	// Legacy headless callers do not use the session manager.

		if backend_drain_pty(active_b) > 0 {
			any_damaged = true
		}
	}
	threaded := backend_is_threaded(active_b)
	active_term := &active_b.front_terminal if threaded else &active_b.terminal

	if _damage_cells(active_term) > 0 {
		any_damaged = true
	}

		any_damaged = true
	}
	_app_drain_terminal_events(a, active_term)

	// Window title sync from backend / interaction HUD
	_app_sync_title(a)

	cursor_changed, position_changed, style_changed, old_row, old_col := backend_sync_cursor(active_b)
	if cursor_changed || position_changed {
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, old_row, old_col)
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(active_term)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	} else if style_changed {
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(active_term)
		_app_mark_cursor_dirty(a, cur.row, cur.col)
	}

	dt_ms := _app_frame_dt_ms()
	dt_sec := dt_ms / 1000.0

	drop_fx_anim_active, drop_fx_changed := drop_fx_tick(&a.drop_fx, dt_sec)
	if drop_fx_changed {
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
	}

	anim_active := a.tab_bar.anim.anim_active
	if platform_tabs.tabs_anim_update(&a.tab_bar, a.session_mgr.active_idx, dt_ms, a.ui_theme.motion.hover_ms, a.ui_theme.motion.active_ms) {
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		anim_active = true
	}
	if drop_fx_anim_active {
		a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
		anim_active = true
	}

	is_dirty := any_damaged || n_events > 0 || a.renderer.full_redraw_pending || anim_active || a.has_deferred_render
	if is_dirty {
		should_render := true
		now_ns := u64(platform.platform_ticks_to_ns(platform.platform_now()))
		if a.target_frame_interval_ns > 0 && a.window.handle != nil {
			elapsed_ns := now_ns - a.last_present_time_ns if now_ns > a.last_present_time_ns else a.target_frame_interval_ns
			if !has_keyboard_input && a.last_present_time_ns > 0 && elapsed_ns < a.target_frame_interval_ns {
				should_render = false
				a.has_deferred_render = true
			}
		}

		if should_render {
			a.has_deferred_render = false
			if threaded {
				backend_lock_render(active_b)
				a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = _app_render_unlock
				a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = rawptr(active_b)
			}
			state := backend_get_render_state(active_b)
			state.debug_frames = a.debug_frames
			_app_stage_ui(a)
			_ = frontend_render(&a.frontend, &state)
			if threaded {
				a.frontend.renderer.unlock_cb = nil
				a.frontend.renderer.unlock_data = nil
				backend_unlock_render(active_b)
			}
			a.last_present_time_ns = u64(platform.platform_ticks_to_ns(platform.platform_now()))
		}
	}

[988f2260-f1ea-436e-ac27-a57f070bfcfd] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/frontend.odin
Add frontend_apply_vibrancy definition after frontend_apply_theme
--- REPLACEMENT:
// frontend_apply_theme updates the renderer's theme.
frontend_apply_theme :: proc(f: ^Frontend, theme: termgrid.Theme) {
	if f == nil {
		return
	}
	f.renderer.theme = theme
}

// frontend_apply_vibrancy configures window opacity and blur vibrancy dynamically.
frontend_apply_vibrancy :: proc(f: ^Frontend, opacity: f32, blur: bool) {
	if f == nil do return
	if f.window.handle != nil {
		win.window_configure_vibrancy(&f.window, opacity, blur)
	}
	when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
		if rawptr(f.surface) != nil {
			metal_surf := (^metal_backend.Metal_Surface)(rawptr(f.surface))
			metal_backend.configure_surface_vibrancy(metal_surf, opacity, blur)
		}
	}
}
--- TARGET:
// frontend_apply_theme updates the renderer's theme.
frontend_apply_theme :: proc(f: ^Frontend, theme: termgrid.Theme) {
	if f == nil {
		return
	}
	f.renderer.theme = theme
}

	has_deferred_render:      bool,
}

pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^odin_thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [MAX_TABS + 2]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
				if count < len(pfds) {
					pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
					count += 1
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}

		pty_count := count
		ack_idx := -1
		if a.drain_ack_r >= 0 && count < len(pfds) {
			pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = a.drain_ack_r, events = {.IN}}
			ack_idx = count
			count += 1
		}

		if count == 0 {
			time.sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
			continue
		}

		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 10)
		if r > 0 {
			if ack_idx >= 0 && (pfds[ack_idx].revents & {.IN}) != {} {
				buf: [64]byte
				for {
					n := posix.read(a.drain_ack_r, raw_data(buf[:]), len(buf))
					if n <= 0 do break
				}
			}

			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< pty_count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				} else {
					time.sleep(2 * time.Millisecond)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
--- TARGET:
	pty_monitor_thread:  ^odin_thread.Thread,
	pty_monitor_running: b32,
	pty_event_pending:   b32,
	pending_wake_event:  sdl3.Event,
	has_wake_event:      bool,
	frame_probe:              probe.Frame_Probe,
	alloc_probe:              probe.Alloc_Probe,
	last_present_time_ns:     u64,
	target_frame_interval_ns: u64,
	has_deferred_render:      bool,
}

pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^odin_thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [MAX_TABS + 1]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
				if count < len(pfds) {
					pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
					count += 1
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}

			time.sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
			continue
		}

					break

					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY

		}
	}

// app_destroy tears down Session Manager and Frontend in order.
app_destroy :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil {
		return
	}
	if a.pty_monitor_thread != nil {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_monitor_running, false)
		if a.drain_ack_w >= 0 {
			wake_byte: [1]byte = {1}
			_ = posix.write(a.drain_ack_w, raw_data(wake_byte[:]), 1)
		}
		odin_thread.join(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		odin_thread.destroy(a.pty_monitor_thread)
		a.pty_monitor_thread = nil
	}
	if a.drain_ack_r >= 0 {
		posix.close(a.drain_ack_r)
		a.drain_ack_r = -1
	}
	if a.drain_ack_w >= 0 {
		posix.close(a.drain_ack_w)
		a.drain_ack_w = -1
	}
	sdl3.RemoveEventWatch(_app_event_watch, a)
--- TARGET:

	sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, false)

	if a.pty_monitor_thread != nil {
		a.pty_monitor_thread.data = a

[9fb1c4f3-c400-4b13-8e0e-8cd767b0f57b] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update pty_monitor_proc to monitor leaf panes and app_active_backend to resolve focused pane backend
--- REPLACEMENT:
pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^odin_thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [64]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all leaf panes in all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			leaves: [MAX_PANE_NODES]^Pane_Node
			leaf_count := tab_leaf_panes(s, leaves[:])
			if leaf_count > 0 {
				for j in 0 ..< leaf_count {
					leaf := leaves[j]
					if leaf.backend != nil && leaf.backend.pty.state == .Running && leaf.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
						if count < len(pfds) - 1 {
							pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(leaf.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
							count += 1
						}
					}
				}
			} else {
				if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
					if count < len(pfds) - 1 {
						pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
						count += 1
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}

		pty_count := count
		ack_idx := -1
		if a.drain_ack_r >= 0 && count < len(pfds) {
			pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = a.drain_ack_r, events = {.IN}}
			ack_idx = count
			count += 1
		}

		if count == 0 {
			time.sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
			continue
		}

		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 10)
		if r > 0 {
			if ack_idx >= 0 && (pfds[ack_idx].revents & {.IN}) != {} {
				buf: [64]byte
				for {
					n := posix.read(a.drain_ack_r, raw_data(buf[:]), len(buf))
					if n <= 0 do break
				}
			}

			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< pty_count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				} else {
					time.sleep(2 * time.Millisecond)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

_app_on_backend_data_ready :: proc(user_data: rawptr) {
	a := (^App)(user_data)
	if a == nil do return
	if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
		user_ev: sdl3.Event
		user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
		_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
	}
}


// app_active_backend resolves the currently active session backend.
app_active_backend :: proc(a: ^App) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		if b := tab_active_backend(tab); b != nil {
			return b
		}
		return &tab.backend
	}
	return &a.backend
}
--- TARGET:
pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^odin_thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [MAX_TABS + 2]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
				if count < len(pfds) {
					pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
					count += 1
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}

		pty_count := count
		ack_idx := -1
		if a.drain_ack_r >= 0 && count < len(pfds) {
			pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = a.drain_ack_r, events = {.IN}}
			ack_idx = count
			count += 1
		}

		if count == 0 {
			time.sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
			continue
		}

		r := posix.poll(&pfds[0], posix.nfds_t(count), 10)
		if r > 0 {
			if ack_idx >= 0 && (pfds[ack_idx].revents & {.IN}) != {} {
				buf: [64]byte
				for {
					n := posix.read(a.drain_ack_r, raw_data(buf[:]), len(buf))
					if n <= 0 do break
				}
			}

			has_data := false
			for i in 0 ..< pty_count {
				if (pfds[i].revents & {.IN, .HUP, .ERR}) != {} {
					has_data = true
					break
				}
			}
			if has_data {
				if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
					sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
					user_ev: sdl3.Event
					user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
					_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
				} else {
					time.sleep(2 * time.Millisecond)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

_app_on_backend_data_ready :: proc(user_data: rawptr) {
	a := (^App)(user_data)
	if a == nil do return
	if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
		user_ev: sdl3.Event
		user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
		_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
	}
}


// app_active_backend resolves the currently active session backend.
app_active_backend :: proc(a: ^App) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		return &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx].backend
	}
	return &a.backend
}

// frontend_render stages the cursor and presents a frame from the Render_State snapshot.
frontend_render :: proc(f: ^Frontend, state: ^Render_State, panes: []render.Pane_Viewport = nil) -> bool {
	if f == nil || state == nil || state.terminal == nil {
		return false
	}

	if f.use_pinnacle && f.p_engine != nil {
		pinnacle_app.update_from_damage(&f.p_term_adapter, state.terminal, &state.terminal.damage)
		pure_ui.engine_tick(f.p_engine)
		return true
	}

	// Stage cursor overlay before frame acquisition
	if state.cursor != nil && state.view != nil {
		active_offset_x: f32 = 0
		active_offset_y: f32 = 0
		if panes != nil && len(panes) > 0 {
			for p in panes {
				if p.is_active {
					active_offset_x = p.x - f.renderer.pad_x
					active_offset_y = p.y - f.renderer.pad_y
					break
				}
			}
		}
		_ = render.cursor_overlay_draw(&f.renderer, state.cursor, state.view.scrollback_offset, active_offset_x, active_offset_y)
	}

	// Stage interaction overlay before frame acquisition
	if state.interaction != nil && state.view != nil && state.terminal != nil {
		_ = render.interaction_overlay_draw(&f.renderer, state.interaction, state.view, state.terminal)
	}

	// Stage scrollbar overlay before frame acquisition
	if state.terminal != nil && (panes == nil || len(panes) <= 1) {
		total_lines := termgrid.scrollback_len(&state.terminal.scrollback) + state.terminal.grid.row_count
		visible_lines := state.terminal.grid.row_count
		offset := state.view.scrollback_offset if state.view != nil else 0
		viewport_w := f32(f.renderer.surface_w)
		viewport_h := f32(f.renderer.surface_h)
		termgrid.scrollbar_update(&state.terminal.scrollbar, total_lines, visible_lines, offset, viewport_w, viewport_h)
		_ = render.scrollbar_overlay_draw(&f.renderer, &state.terminal.scrollbar)
	}

	dbg_dmg, dbg_total := 0, 0
	if f.debug_frames || state.debug_frames {
		inp := render.strategy_estimate_inputs(&state.terminal.damage, f.renderer.rows, f.renderer.cols)
		dbg_dmg, dbg_total = inp.dirty_cells, inp.total_cells
	}

	now := platform.platform_ticks_to_ns(platform.platform_now())
	now_ns := u64(now)
	frame_ok := false
	if panes != nil && len(panes) > 1 {
		frame_ok = render.renderer_frame_panes(&f.renderer, panes)
	} else if state.synchronized_output {
		if now_ns - state.sync_output_start_ns < APP_SYNC_OUTPUT_TIMEOUT_NS {
			// Skip calling renderer_frame for this frame (synchronized output in progress)
		} else {
			// Safety timeout reached — force presentation
			frame_ok = render.renderer_frame(&f.renderer, state.terminal, state.view)
		}
	} else {
		frame_ok = render.renderer_frame(&f.renderer, state.terminal, state.view)
	}
--- TARGET:
// frontend_render stages the cursor and presents a frame from the Render_State snapshot.
frontend_render :: proc(f: ^Frontend, state: ^Render_State) -> bool {
	if f == nil || state == nil || state.terminal == nil {
		return false
	}

	if f.use_pinnacle && f.p_engine != nil {

// _app_stage_pane_tree_chrome extracts hairline dividers, active border, and inactive cursors.
_app_stage_pane_tree_chrome :: proc(a: ^App, tree: ^Pane_Tree) {
	if a == nil || tree == nil || tree.root == nil do return
	if tree.node_count <= 1 || tree.zoomed_pane_id != 0 do return

	scale := frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend)

	// 1. Collect hairline dividers from Split nodes
	dividers: [MAX_PANE_NODES]ui.Pane_Divider
	div_count := 0
	for i in 0 ..< MAX_PANE_NODES {
		if !tree.node_in_use[i] do continue
		node := &tree.nodes[i]
		if node.kind != .Split || node.first == nil do continue

		is_hov := (tree.divider_hover == node)
		switch node.direction {
		case .Vertical:
			div_x := node.first.rect.x + node.first.rect.w
			div_y := node.rect.y
			dividers[div_count] = ui.Pane_Divider{
				x = div_x,
				y = div_y,
				w = DIVIDER_SIZE,
				h = node.rect.h,
				is_hovered = is_hov,
			}
			div_count += 1
		case .Horizontal:
			div_x := node.rect.x
			div_y := node.first.rect.y + node.first.rect.h
			dividers[div_count] = ui.Pane_Divider{
				x = div_x,
				y = div_y,
				w = node.rect.w,
				h = DIVIDER_SIZE,
				is_hovered = is_hov,
			}
			div_count += 1
		}
	}

	// 2. Active pane outline
	active_rect: ui.Rect_f32
	show_active_border := false
	if focused := pane_tree_find_pane(tree, tree.focused_pane_id); focused != nil {
		active_rect = focused.rect
		show_active_border = true
	}

	ui.ui_stage_pane_chrome(&a.renderer, &a.ui_theme, dividers[:div_count], active_rect, show_active_border, scale)

	// 3. Hollow box cursor [▯] for inactive panes
	cell_w := a.renderer.cell_width > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_width : f32(APP_CELL_W)
	cell_h := a.renderer.cell_height > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_height : f32(APP_CELL_H)
	log_cw := cell_w / scale
	log_ch := cell_h / scale

	for i in 0 ..< MAX_PANE_NODES {
		if !tree.node_in_use[i] do continue
		node := &tree.nodes[i]
		if node.kind != .Leaf || node.id == tree.focused_pane_id || node.backend == nil do continue

		b := node.backend
		t := &b.front_terminal if backend_is_threaded(b) else &b.terminal
		cur := termgrid.terminal_get_cursor(t)
		if cur.visible && cur.row >= 0 && cur.row < node.rows && cur.col >= 0 && cur.col < node.cols {
			cx := node.rect.x + FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING + f32(cur.col) * log_cw
			cy := node.rect.y + FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING + f32(cur.row) * log_ch
			ui.ui_stage_hollow_cursor(&a.renderer, cx, cy, log_cw, log_ch, [4]f32{0.75, 0.75, 0.75, 0.75}, scale)
		}
	}
}

_app_stage_ui :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil || a.window.handle == nil do return
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	ui_tabs: [MAX_TABS]platform_tabs.Tab_Info
	tab_count := _app_build_ui_tabs(a, ui_tabs[:])
	_ = ui.ui_render_stage(
		&a.renderer,
		&a.ui_theme,
		i18n.i18n_get(),
		&a.tab_bar,
		ui_tabs[:tab_count],
		a.tab_rects[:tab_count],
		&a.search_bar,
		f32(a.window.width) if a.window.width > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_w),
		f32(a.window.height) if a.window.height > 0 else f32(a.window.pixel_h),
		frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend),
		a.base_title,
		&a.tab_drag,
		&a.tab_menu,
		&a.tab_rename,
		&a.confirm_dialog,
		&a.tab_overflow,
		&a.session_switcher,
	)

	if a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		active_tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		_app_stage_pane_tree_chrome(a, &active_tab.tree)
	}

	// One shared logical-space surface; the shader applies the Retina scale once.
	waves: [instance.WATER_MAX_WAVES]instance.Water_Wave
	count := 0

--- TARGET:
	if a.session_switcher.visible {
		platform_tabs.session_switcher_layout(&a.session_switcher, window_w, window_h)
	}
	_app_set_drag_region(a)
}

[9fb1c4f3-c400-4b13-8e0e-8cd767b0f57b] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin

// _app_spawn_pane_backend creates and initializes a new backend for a split pane.
_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
	cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
	b := new(Backend)
	if !backend_init(b, rows, cols, shell, shell_argv, &a.config, a.renderer.theme) {
		free(b)
		return nil
	}
	backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
	backend_set_notify_data_ready(b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
	_ = backend_start_thread(b)
	return b
}

[9fb1c4f3-c400-4b13-8e0e-8cd767b0f57b] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update _app_pointer_cell to handle active pane rectangle and cell size relative conversion
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_pointer_cell :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	if a == nil {
		return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}

	tab := session_active_tab(&a.session_mgr)
	if tab != nil && tab.tree.root != nil {
		focused := tab_active_pane(tab)
		if focused != nil && focused.rect.w > 0 && focused.rect.h > 0 {
			scale := frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend)
			cell_w := a.renderer.cell_width > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_width / scale : f32(APP_CELL_W)
			cell_h := a.renderer.cell_height > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_height / scale : f32(APP_CELL_H)
			rel_x := x - focused.rect.x - FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING
			rel_y := y - focused.rect.y - FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING
			col := 0
			if rel_x > 0 && cell_w > 0 {
				col = int(rel_x / cell_w)
			}
			row := 0
			if rel_y > 0 && cell_h > 0 {
				row = int(rel_y / cell_h)
			}
			return termgrid.Terminal_Point{
				col = clamp(col, 0, max(0, b.terminal.grid.col_count - 1)),
				row = clamp(row, 0, max(0, b.terminal.grid.row_count - 1)),
			}
		}
	}

	return frontend_pointer_cell(&a.frontend, b.terminal.grid.row_count, b.terminal.grid.col_count, x, y)
}
--- TARGET:
_app_pointer_cell :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	if a == nil {
		return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	return frontend_pointer_cell(&a.frontend, b.terminal.grid.row_count, b.terminal.grid.col_count, x, y)
}

[c241bd2f-68aa-45df-b641-3f457879784f] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update _app_spawn_pane_backend signature and row/col initialization
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App, init_rows: int = 0, init_cols: int = 0) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	rows := init_rows if init_rows > 0 else (a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS)
	cols := init_cols if init_cols > 0 else (a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS)
	b := new(Backend)
	if !backend_init(b, rows, cols, shell, shell_argv, &a.config, a.renderer.theme) {
		free(b)
		return nil
	}
	backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
	backend_set_notify_data_ready(b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
	_ = backend_start_thread(b)
	return b
}
--- TARGET:
_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	rows := a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS
	cols := a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS
	b := new(Backend)
	if !backend_init(b, rows, cols, shell, shell_argv, &a.config, a.renderer.theme) {
		free(b)
		return nil
	}
	backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
	backend_set_notify_data_ready(b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
	_ = backend_start_thread(b)
	return b

						new_b := _app_spawn_pane_backend(a, t_rows, t_cols)
						if new_b != nil {
							new_pane_id, split_ok := pane_tree_split(&active_tab.tree, active_tab.tree.focused_pane_id, .Horizontal, new_b)
							if !split_ok || new_pane_id == 0 {
								backend_destroy(new_b)
								free(new_b)
							} else {
								_app_layout_ui(a)
								a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
							}
						}
					}
					continue
				}

						active_tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
						focused := tab_active_pane(active_tab)
						t_rows := 0
						t_cols := 0
						if focused != nil && focused.rows > 0 && focused.cols > 0 {
							t_rows = max(MIN_ROWS, focused.rows)
							t_cols = max(MIN_COLS, (focused.cols - 1) / 2)
						}
						new_b := _app_spawn_pane_backend(a, t_rows, t_cols)
						if new_b != nil {
							new_pane_id, split_ok := pane_tree_split(&active_tab.tree, active_tab.tree.focused_pane_id, .Vertical, new_b)
							if !split_ok || new_pane_id == 0 {
								backend_destroy(new_b)
								free(new_b)
							} else {
								_app_layout_ui(a)
								a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
							}
						}
					}
					continue
				}
--- TARGET:

[c241bd2f-68aa-45df-b641-3f457879784f] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update _app_pointer_cell and _app_route_pointer to target the hit leaf pane
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_pointer_cell :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	if a == nil {
		return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}

	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		if tab.tree.root != nil {
			hit_leaf, _ := pane_tree_hit_test(&tab.tree, x, y)
			target_pane := hit_leaf if hit_leaf != nil else tab_active_pane(tab)
			if target_pane != nil && target_pane.rect.w > 0 && target_pane.rect.h > 0 {
				target_b := target_pane.backend if target_pane.backend != nil else b
				scale := frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend)
				cell_w := a.renderer.cell_width > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_width / scale : f32(APP_CELL_W)
				cell_h := a.renderer.cell_height > 0 ? a.renderer.cell_height / scale : f32(APP_CELL_H)
				rel_x := x - target_pane.rect.x - FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING
				rel_y := y - target_pane.rect.y - FRONTEND_CONTENT_PADDING
				col := 0
				if rel_x > 0 && cell_w > 0 {
					col = int(rel_x / cell_w)
				}
				row := 0
				if rel_y > 0 && cell_h > 0 {
					row = int(rel_y / cell_h)
				}
				return termgrid.Terminal_Point{
					col = clamp(col, 0, max(0, target_b.terminal.grid.col_count - 1)),
					row = clamp(row, 0, max(0, target_b.terminal.grid.row_count - 1)),
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return frontend_pointer_cell(&a.frontend, b.terminal.grid.row_count, b.terminal.grid.col_count, x, y)
}

_app_pointer_point :: proc(a: ^App, x, y: f32) -> termgrid.Terminal_Point {
	viewport := _app_pointer_cell(a, x, y)
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) > 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		if tab.tree.root != nil {
			hit_leaf, _ := pane_tree_hit_test(&tab.tree, x, y)
			if hit_leaf != nil && hit_leaf.backend != nil {
				b = hit_leaf.backend
			}
		}
	}
	if b == nil do return termgrid.Terminal_Point{}
	return termgrid.terminal_view_point_from_viewport(
		&b.terminal,
		&b.view,
		viewport,
	)
}

_app_pointer_wheel_delta :: backend_pointer_wheel_delta
_app_pointer_selection_changed :: backend_pointer_selection_changed

		tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		if tab.tree.root != nil {
			hit_leaf, _ := pane_tree_hit_test(&tab.tree, pointer.x, pointer.y)
			if hit_leaf != nil && hit_leaf.backend != nil {
				b = hit_leaf.backend
			}
		}
	}
	if b == nil do return false

[c241bd2f-68aa-45df-b641-3f457879784f] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add _app_lock_rendered_panes, _app_unlock_rendered_panes, and _app_render_unlock_all_panes
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_render_unlock :: proc(data: rawptr) {
	b := (^Backend)(data)
	if b != nil {
		backend_unlock_render(b)
	}
}

_app_lock_rendered_panes :: proc(active_tab: ^Tab_Session) {
	if active_tab == nil do return
	leaves: [MAX_PANE_NODES]^Pane_Node
	n := tab_leaf_panes(active_tab, leaves[:])
	for i in 0 ..< n {
		if leaves[i] != nil && leaves[i].backend != nil && backend_is_threaded(leaves[i].backend) {
			backend_lock_render(leaves[i].backend)
		}
	}
}

_app_unlock_rendered_panes :: proc(active_tab: ^Tab_Session) {
	if active_tab == nil do return
	leaves: [MAX_PANE_NODES]^Pane_Node
	n := tab_leaf_panes(active_tab, leaves[:])
	for i in 0 ..< n {
		if leaves[i] != nil && leaves[i].backend != nil && backend_is_threaded(leaves[i].backend) {
			backend_unlock_render(leaves[i].backend)
		}
	}
}

_app_render_unlock_all_panes :: proc(data: rawptr) {
	tab := (^Tab_Session)(data)
	_app_unlock_rendered_panes(tab)
}
--- TARGET:
_app_render_unlock :: proc(data: rawptr) {
	b := (^Backend)(data)
	if b != nil {
		backend_unlock_render(b)
	}
}

Add pty_monitor_mtx to App struct and lock it around tabs iteration in pty_monitor_proc
--- REPLACEMENT:
	drag_region:         win.Drag_Region,

pty_monitor_proc :: proc(t: ^odin_thread.Thread) {
	a := (^App)(t.data)
	if a == nil do return

	pfds: [64]posix.pollfd

	for sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_monitor_running) {
		count := 0

		sync.mutex_lock(&a.pty_monitor_mtx)
		// Gather active PTY master file descriptors across all leaf panes in all tabs
		for i in 0 ..< len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
			s := &a.session_mgr.tabs[i]
			leaves: [MAX_PANE_NODES]^Pane_Node
			leaf_count := tab_leaf_panes(s, leaves[:])
			if leaf_count > 0 {
				for j in 0 ..< leaf_count {
					leaf := leaves[j]
					if leaf.backend != nil && leaf.backend.pty.state == .Running && leaf.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
						if count < len(pfds) - 1 {
							pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(leaf.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
							count += 1
						}
					}
				}
			} else {
				if s.backend.pty.state == .Running && s.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
					if count < len(pfds) - 1 {
						pfds[count] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(s.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
						count += 1
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Also check single backend fallback if no tabs
		if count == 0 && a.backend.pty.state == .Running && a.backend.pty.master >= 0 {
			pfds[0] = posix.pollfd{fd = posix.FD(a.backend.pty.master), events = {.IN}}
			count = 1
		}
		sync.mutex_unlock(&a.pty_monitor_mtx)

[7e67345d-ed10-4df1-9981-0027b9b051a0] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add _app_wake_pty_monitor proc
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_on_backend_data_ready :: proc(user_data: rawptr) {
	a := (^App)(user_data)
	if a == nil do return
	if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
		user_ev: sdl3.Event
		user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
		_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
	}
}

_app_wake_pty_monitor :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a != nil && a.drain_ack_w >= 0 {
		wake_byte: [1]byte = {1}
		_ = posix.write(a.drain_ack_w, raw_data(wake_byte[:]), 1)
	}
}

--- TARGET:
_app_on_backend_data_ready :: proc(user_data: rawptr) {
	a := (^App)(user_data)
	if a == nil do return
	if !sync.atomic_load(&a.pty_event_pending) {
		sync.atomic_store(&a.pty_event_pending, true)
		user_ev: sdl3.Event
		user_ev.type = PTY_DATA_READY
		_ = sdl3.PushEvent(&user_ev)
	}
}

// _app_close_tab_confirmed closes the tab at idx and rebinds the chrome modals
// that pointed at it. Closing the final tab requests application quit.
_app_close_tab_confirmed :: proc(a: ^App, idx: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(a.session_mgr.tabs) do return
	_ = session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
	_app_wake_pty_monitor(a)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
}

// _app_spawn_pane_backend creates and initializes a new backend for a split pane.
_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App, init_rows: int = 0, init_cols: int = 0) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	rows := init_rows if init_rows > 0 else (a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS)
	cols := init_cols if init_cols > 0 else (a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS)
	b := new(Backend)
	if !backend_init(b, rows, cols, shell, shell_argv, &a.config, a.renderer.theme) {
		free(b)
		return nil
	}
	backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
	backend_set_notify_data_ready(b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
	_ = backend_start_thread(b)
	_app_wake_pty_monitor(a)
	return b
}
--- TARGET:
		new_idx, spawn_ok := session_spawn(&a.session_mgr, shell, shell_argv, rows, cols, &a.config, a.renderer.theme)
		if spawn_ok {
			new_b := &a.session_mgr.tabs[new_idx].backend
			backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(new_b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
			backend_set_notify_data_ready(new_b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
			session_switch_tab(&a.session_mgr, new_idx)

	case .Rename:
		_app_begin_tab_rename(a, target_idx)
	}
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
	platform_tabs.tab_bar_anim_activate(&a.tab_bar)
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
}

// _app_close_tab_confirmed closes the tab at idx and rebinds the chrome modals
// that pointed at it. Closing the final tab requests application quit.
_app_close_tab_confirmed :: proc(a: ^App, idx: int) {
	if a == nil do return
	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(a.session_mgr.tabs) do return
	_ = session_close_tab(&a.session_mgr, idx)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true
	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
}

// _app_spawn_pane_backend creates and initializes a new backend for a split pane.
_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App, init_rows: int = 0, init_cols: int = 0) -> ^Backend {
	if a == nil do return nil
	shell, shell_allocated := _resolve_shell()
	defer if shell_allocated { delete(shell) }
	shell_argv := _resolve_shell_argv(shell)
	rows := init_rows if init_rows > 0 else (a.renderer.rows > 0 ? int(a.renderer.rows) : APP_DEFAULT_ROWS)
	cols := init_cols if init_cols > 0 else (a.renderer.cols > 0 ? int(a.renderer.cols) : APP_DEFAULT_COLS)
	b := new(Backend)
	if !backend_init(b, rows, cols, shell, shell_argv, &a.config, a.renderer.theme) {
		free(b)
		return nil
	}
	backend_set_clipboard_callbacks(b, &a.frontend, _frontend_clipboard_write_cb, _frontend_clipboard_read_cb)
	backend_set_notify_data_ready(b, a, _app_on_backend_data_ready)
	_ = backend_start_thread(b)
	return b
}

_app_spawn_pane_backend :: proc(a: ^App, init_rows: int = 0, init_cols: int = 0, target_cwd: string = "") -> ^Backend {

// _app_open_session_switcher populates active tabs, detached sessions, and saved layouts.
_app_open_session_switcher :: proc(a: ^App) {
	if a == nil do return
	reg := a.session_mgr.registry if a.session_mgr.registry != nil else session_core.session_registry_default()
	saved := persistence_list_available()
	defer {
		for s in saved do delete(s)
		delete(saved)
	}
	platform_tabs.session_switcher_show(&a.session_switcher, a.session_mgr.tabs[:], a.session_mgr.active_idx, reg, saved[:])
}
--- TARGET:
	_app_wake_pty_monitor(a)
	if len(a.session_mgr.tabs) == 0 do a.should_quit = true

	_app_resync_tab_modals(a)
}

[c8ff4719-3666-43cf-a18d-5ea7c0753af6] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Add CLI --restore flag processing right after app_init in main proc
--- REPLACEMENT:
	app := new(App, runtime.heap_allocator())
	if !app_init(app, APP_DEFAULT_ROWS, APP_DEFAULT_COLS, shell, shell_argv) {
		free(app, runtime.heap_allocator())
		fmt.println("ERROR: app_init failed")
		return
	}
	defer {
		app_destroy(app)
		free(app, runtime.heap_allocator())
	}

	// Check for --restore / -r / -p / --layout CLI flags
	restore_layout_name := ""
	should_restore := false
	for i := 1; i < len(os.args); i += 1 {
		arg := os.args[i]
		if arg == "--restore" || arg == "-r" || arg == "-p" || arg == "--layout" {
			should_restore = true
			if i + 1 < len(os.args) && !strings.has_prefix(os.args[i + 1], "-") {
				restore_layout_name = os.args[i + 1]
				i += 1
			} else {
				restore_layout_name = DEFAULT_LAYOUT_NAME
			}
			break
		}
	}

	if should_restore {
		layout, ok := persistence_load_layout(restore_layout_name)
		if ok {
			new_idx, rok := persistence_restore_tab(app, layout)
			if rok {
				if len(app.session_mgr.tabs) > 1 {
					_ = session_close_tab(&app.session_mgr, 0)
					session_switch_tab(&app.session_mgr, 0)
				} else {
					session_switch_tab(&app.session_mgr, new_idx)
				}
				_app_resync_tab_modals(app)
			}
			persistence_free_layout(&layout)
		} else {
			fmt.eprintf("[term] Warning: layout '%s' not found across search paths\n", restore_layout_name)
		}
	}

[b7d306c3-800a-4607-b677-cffbb5eda4ae] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Update _app_apply_zoom to rebuild font and trigger resize safely without mutating terminal or pty from main thread
--- REPLACEMENT:
_app_apply_zoom :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false

	if !backend_is_threaded(b) {
		return frontend_apply_zoom(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty)
	}

	if b.pty.state == .Exited || a.frontend.zoom_target_logical_size <= 0 {
		return false
	}
	target := a.frontend.zoom_target_logical_size
	if target == a.frontend.logical_font_size {
		a.frontend.zoom_target_logical_size = 0
		return false
	}

	scale := frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend)
	physical := target * scale
	fallback_candidates := frontend_font_paths(a.frontend.executable_path, FALLBACK_FONT_PATHS, context.temp_allocator)
	defer frontend_font_paths_destroy(fallback_candidates, context.temp_allocator)
	if !render.renderer_rebuild_font(
		&a.frontend.renderer,
		a.frontend.font_path,
		physical,
		fallback_candidates,
	) {
		a.frontend.zoom_target_logical_size = 0
		return false
	}

	a.frontend.logical_font_size = target
	a.frontend.physical_font_size = physical
	a.frontend.zoom_target_logical_size = 0

	frontend_update_padding(&a.frontend)

	a.last_px_w = 0
	a.last_px_h = 0
	a.renderer.full_redraw_pending = true

	if a.session_mgr.active_idx >= 0 && a.session_mgr.active_idx < len(a.session_mgr.tabs) {
		active_tab := &a.session_mgr.tabs[a.session_mgr.active_idx]
		leaves: [MAX_PANE_NODES]^Pane_Node
		leaf_count := tab_leaf_panes(active_tab, leaves[:])
		for i in 0 ..< leaf_count {
			leaf := leaves[i]
			if leaf != nil {
				leaf.dispatched_rows = 0
				leaf.dispatched_cols = 0
			}
		}
	}

	pixel_w := a.window.pixel_w
	pixel_h := a.window.pixel_h
	if pixel_w <= 0 {
		pixel_w = i32(a.renderer.screen_w)
	}
	if pixel_h <= 0 {
		pixel_h = i32(a.renderer.screen_h)
	}

	app_on_resize(a, pixel_w, pixel_h)
	_app_layout_ui(a)
	return true
}
--- TARGET:
_app_apply_zoom :: proc(a: ^App) -> bool {
	if a == nil {
		return false
	}
	b := app_active_backend(a)
	if b == nil do return false
	return frontend_apply_zoom(&a.frontend, &b.terminal, &b.pty)
}

[1b3ac6bb-1ff4-47f2-977f-0ec18b5f890a] PATCH TO /Users/dwlhm/project/term/src/app/main.odin
Calculate target logical dimensions, resize window, flush events, query correct pixel size, show window, and then query grid dimensions for PTY.
--- REPLACEMENT:
	// (2) Pre-emptive DPI Scaling: Ensure window is sized EXACTLY for the target dimensions
	// BEFORE spawning the PTY. This prevents a late SIGWINCH from triggering a p10k/zsh flicker.
	scale := frontend_content_scale(&a.frontend)
	target_logical_w := i32( (f32(eff_cols) * cell_w + pad_x * 2.0) / scale + 0.5 )
	target_logical_h := i32( (f32(eff_rows) * cell_h + pad_y * 2.0) / scale + 0.5 )
	win.window_set_size(&a.window, target_logical_w, target_logical_h)
	
	// Force SDL to clear its event queue of transient startup/resize noise
	sdl3.PumpEvents()
	sdl3.FlushEvents(sdl3.EventType.WINDOW_RESIZED, sdl3.EventType.WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED)
	win.window_update_pixel_size(&a.window)

	// Now show the window visually once bounds are totally resolved.
	win.window_show(&a.window)

	init_rows, init_cols := grid_dimensions_for_pixels(
		a.window.pixel_w, a.window.pixel_h, cell_w, cell_h, pad_x, pad_y,
	)

	init_rows, init_cols := grid_dimensions_for_pixels(

	)
